Absolute sequence lengths stay monotonic; append and reader offsets map into the ring, with the aligned live prefix rebased contiguously at wrap so the audited prime/decode/batched/verify/MTP views keep their existing address contract. With the door open, explicit prime chunks are capped at 4096 (`0` becomes 4096); with it closed, the legacy explicit schedule is unchanged. Flat-history prefix snapshots/restores/fanout are refused with a log line, and lapped affinity/spec rewinds decline instead of fabricating overwritten rows. DC/graph paths remain refused for Step35 by construction. **Validated serving-config opt-in:** `ringval-20260810` is COMPLETE with wrap byte-identity, a one-hash golden, `run-spec` K=1..8, and 2 -> 12 simultaneous 262k sessions (6.0x); `newboxgates-20260811` reproduces 2 -> 12 on the new 2x RTX PRO 6000 Workstation Edition pair. Default remains OFF. Remaining doors are the unmodified `serve-smoke` prefix-cache cell, **SCOPED RED** because ring sessions deliberately refuse flat-history prefix-cache operations, and the default-flip policy. Receipts: `research/ringval-20260810/RESULTS.md`, `research/newboxgates-20260811/RESULTS.md` |
| `MEMRA_FA_SMEM_TKV` | 1024 | t_kv crossover to the smem-broadcast FA decode twin (`0` = never). Swept 2026-07-05: flat 512–2048 on real prompts; micro says lower may pay on the 35B (untested e2e arm: 96). fp8 layer classes (gemma GKV/WKV) are excluded — the smem V-stage is q5_1-hardcoded |
| `MEMRA_FA_SPW` | 32 | gemma windowed-attention split width. Re-swept 2026-07-12 under the raw-e4m3 sV occupancy ceiling (t=1 decode is grid-limited): 32 wins plain (+4 at 1.7k vs 48); SPEC serving sets `64` (verify fills the grid via rows, bigger splits amortize staging — depth K6 301 vs 249 at 32). MUST be uniform across decode/verify (a t-keyed probe broke combine-order parity: stream 9/128). The default is tuned for the 82-SM 5090 Laptop; the optimum tracks SM count (a 188-SM RTX PRO 6000 measured +4% at `24`, 2026-07-20) — on bigger GPUs, sweep {16,24,32,48,64} |
| `MEMRA_FA_SP512` | 16 | gemma global (hd512) split override — occupancy for the nkv=2 dpl16 grid (+5.7% depth spec when landed; re-swept under e4m3 2026-07-12: 16 stands) |
| `MEMRA_FA512_MIN` | 512 | hd512 vec-lane crossover floor (scalar's more-blocks latency hiding wins below it) |
| `MEMRA_FA_VEC_MIN` | 96 (per-model) | FA vec-lane t_kv floor override (gemma model init lowers it — v4 wins at every depth there) |
| `MEMRA_FA_SPLIT` | ctx-adaptive | force fixed FA split-keys. EXACTNESS: split count changes combine FP order — run-spec must re-gate (32 broke 9B self-consistency, 2026-07-03/07). The adaptive ladder is also SM-COUNT-keyed (`fa_sm_count() >= 128` rung, lib.rs): the same artifact legally produces different near-tie greedy streams on 82-SM vs >=128-SM rigs because the rung picks split 8 vs 16 at low `n_head_kv` — verdict FLIP-NEARTIE, not a defect; at matched split, cross-rig teacher-forced logits are byte-identical (lane/k27-divergence 2026-08-05, `research/k27-divergence-20260805/VERDICT.md`). The contract wording is one canonical output per FA-split config; the k27 fast-gate row pins `MEMRA_FA_SPLIT=8` so its golden is rig-portable, and `k27div-probe` (concat-prime-probe family) is the cross-rig teacher-forced localizer |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK` | 4096 cap; PP-2 auto | chunked-prime nominal chunk size in tokens (`0` = monolithic). When unset on a live PP-2 prime split, the geometry targets up to eight microchunks with a 128-token nominal floor: `min(4096, max(128, ceil(T/8)))` for calls with `T >= 256`. An explicit value remains authoritative and always requests fixed-size ranges. The naked auto range schedule is selected by `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK_SCHED`. PURE long-ctx OOM/transient control since the grain-free fix (lane/chunkinv-flip, 2026-08-05): every chunk — including chunk 0 — attends through the quantized KV cache (quantize-then-attend), so the chunk size no longer steers arithmetic and prefill logits are bit-identical across values (chunkinv gate, naked env) — that fix and gate cover the attention precision class; on chunked-GDN archs a boundary off the `gdn_chunk_size()` WY fold grid remains the documented lawful near-tie class, which is why serve-CHOSEN boundaries are grid-aligned (see `MEMRA_PRIME_GRID_ALIGN`) and, since 2026-08-23 (lane/hermes-perf-fixes), the AUTO internal range boundaries are snapped to the same grid at schedule time (`align_prime_ranges_to_gdn`; `MEMRA_PRIME_GRID_ALIGN=0` is the shared rollback seam; an explicit value keeps its operator-authoritative unaligned fixed ranges). On step35 (Step-3.7-Flash) a SECOND door reopened this per-arch and was closed on 2026-08-07 — see `MEMRA_STEP35_SWA_TKV` and `research/step35-chunkfix-20260807`. History of the class edge it used to move: `research/chunk-invariance-20260805/VERDICT.md` |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK_SCHED` | `dynamic` | PP-2 naked-auto microchunk schedule (lane/cx-dynamic-microchunk, 2026-08-08). `dynamic` keeps the fixed policy's chunk count, emits a short fill chunk, then places the remaining boundaries at equal increments of the linear-heavy cumulative work model `W(L)=L^2+8*T*L`, producing a larger middle followed by a shrinking tail. `fixed` is the rollback to the measured equal-token ranges. Scheduler-only on the classes `ppsplit` pins (attention precision); `chunkinv35`/`tickinv35` remain that authority. Those gates never constrained GDN WY block membership — on chunked-GDN archs BOTH auto schedules now emit grid-aligned internal boundaries (`align_prime_ranges_to_gdn`, 2026-08-23), so the off-grid near-tie class documented at `MEMRA_PRIME_GRID_ALIGN` is reachable only through an explicit `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK`. An explicit `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK` overrides this seam and stays fixed |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_F32CHUNK0` | off | `1` restores the LEGACY first-chunk arithmetic (chunk 0 attends this batch's f32 K/V via fa_prefill instead of the quantized cache) — the rollback seam for the grain-free chunk-invariance fix, and the chunkinv gate's canary injection. Chunk-VARIANT by construction; never on in a measured default run |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_SWA_TKV` | off | `1` restores step35's PRE-FIX SWA prefill arm predicate (kernel keyed on the chunk's own `t_kv > win` instead of the request's `seq_end > win`) — the rollback seam for the step35 chunk-invariance fix, and the `chunkinv35` gate's canary injection. Chunk-VARIANT by construction: it makes the FA rows a prefix `[0,P)` with `P = c*floor(win/c)`, so `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK` moves the logits (measured 1.813e0 at T=4883, greedy text diverging at step 6, with a one-token 512-vs-513 verdict flip). step35 only; never on in a measured default run. `research/step35-chunkfix-20260807`, defect receipt `research/step37-p2-20260806/raw/chunkinv-step35-GAP2-CONFIRMED-20260807.txt` |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_CALLLOCAL` | off | `1` restores the FULL pre-fix tick-segmentation arithmetic: the PER-CALL `seq_end` (`cache.pos + t`, ignoring the caller's `queued_after`) plus the raw, unaligned step35 SWA view offset. Both halves are required after the windowed hd128 FA default: the predicate alone selects between bit-identical FA results while the raw offset restores the call-boundary-dependent tile grid. This is the rollback seam for the tick-segmentation fix and the `tickinv35` gate's canary injection; never on in a measured default run. `research/tick-seg-20260807`, `research/v072-fix1-20260808` |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_GRID_ALIGN` | on | `0` restores the LEGACY serve-chosen prime boundaries (plain/spec checkpoint stop at the raw turn-marker index; prefix-cache LCP-split and message-boundary captures at the raw positions) — the rollback seam for the prime-grid boundary alignment (lane/spec-longctx-20260821) and the box-side long-ctx byte gate's broken arm. Default ON rounds every serve-CHOSEN stop/capture boundary DOWN to `gdn_chunk_size()` (`grid_align_boundary`, worker.rs), because cross-call prime splits on the qwen hybrid are bit-identical to the monolithic prime **iff the split sits on the GDN WY fold grid** (measured at 24k ctx: aligned splits rows_diff 0, EXACT; off-grid splits diverge from exactly the split row with greedy flips at p0.0 near-tie margins — the GATES-SMOKE-20260821 B3/B1-fold class, 13/16 turns at agent lengths). Costs at most `gdn_chunk_size()-1` re-primed suffix tokens per resume. Engine law pinned by `primegrid`/`primegridc` (tools/prime-grid-gate.sh); mechanism receipts `research/multiturn-cache-20260821/LONGCTX-EXACTNESS-20260821.md` (darklanes). Off-grid ENGINE-level splits remain the documented lawful near-tie class; never off in a measured default run |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_SWA_FA` | on | `0` rolls step35's SWA prefill back to the f32 windowed floor (`sdpa_naive_w_quantized_view`) instead of the windowed hd128 FA stamp (`fa_prefill_view_ws_w_hd128`, default since lane/pp-prefill 2026-08-07). The floor was the single largest cost in the pp4096 anatomy — 565 ms x 33 SWA layers = 41% of the prime, while the FA family did the harder causal-4096 in 3.3 ms (`research/pp-prefill-20260807`). Different NUMERIC CLASS (bf16-MMA online softmax vs f32 serial), selected on the request's `seq_end` like every step35 prime arm, so it is chunk-size-invariant by the chunkfix construction. The rollback is the A/B seam and the previous numeric config; never mix arms within one measured comparison |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_BATCH` | on | `0` disables the Step35 batched trunk and caps serving chunks at B=1. On PP-N, B=1 is deliberately refused too: the eager B=1 trunk is a different numeric class from `step35_decode_batch_layers`, and allowing the live scheduler to cross that boundary made greedy bytes depend on load history (`research/p0iso-20260810`, fixed by `research/b1fix-20260810`). An unsplit deployment retains its existing eager B=1 route. Default ON = the real batched arm (`step35_decode_batch_layers`: per-layer n_head/partial-rope/per-session SWA views/head gate at m=B; batched over weights, per-session over KV) serves B=1..8 — decode aggregate 34-flat → **81/96/116/129 tok/s at c=1/2/4/8** before the PP-N correctness default (both PRO 6000 pairs, N=3 medians, `research/step35-batch-20260808`; updated B=1 cost in `research/b1fix-20260810`). Also the `b2geo35c` canary seam: the gate's live assertions must fail under it. Rollback seam only; never off in a measured default run |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_PRIME_BATCH` | on | `0` disables step35's dedicated cross-request **fresh-prompt** concat-prime range walk and restores the named refusal/fallback to serialized single primes. Default ON batches the legacy owning-stage weight-streaming trunk at `m=sum(T)` while keeping each request's step35 attention geometry, absolute `seq_end`, KV append, and cache isolated; under PP-N each layer range runs on its owning stage with the same fence/transport/publication discipline as `prime_chunk_ppn`. When `MEMRA_STEP_TP_PREFILL=1`, the concat path refuses before cache mutation because the TP4 B=2 implementation missed its live-server performance bar; the server restores the queues and runs the proven per-session grouped path instead. Interactive serving drains each eligible fresh prompt in full before this call. Carried/dark-lane continuation batches still refuse and fall back because they need per-request queued-after metadata. Gate: `step35-prime-batch-gate.sh` (`pbatch35`/`pbatch35c`) requires serial byte identity through teacher-forced reads of the primed KV plus live PP-split evidence. `research/primebatch-20260808/` |
| `MEMRA_MOE_VRAM_FRAC` | 0.85 | SLRU expert-cache fraction of free VRAM (sweep 2026-07-06: 0.40=25.0 → 0.85=28.5 tok/s). The measured Hy3 5090 launcher sets 0.90 for both soft and hard fractions; lower it on rigs co-running other GPU work |
| `MEMRA_MOE_HARD_VRAM_FRAC` | 0.80 | machine-specific hard ceiling for the SLRU allocation (`0.10..=0.95`). Raise only after a local OOM-gated sweep. Hy3 on the 24GB RTX 5090 validated `0.85` at 21.21GiB peak framebuffer use: 2,006 slots, exact argmax/tokens, and 0.53 -> 1.03 tok/s on the warm repeat together with worker depth 16 |
| `MEMRA_MOE_SLOTS` | auto | force an exact SLRU slot count (spill experiments used 64/512) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_RESIDENT` | on | `0` forces the SLRU path even when experts fit VRAM (fits-VRAM resident = 169.55 vs 28.5 tok/s on the local 35B) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_RESIDENT_GB` | free − trunk − headroom | resident-experts budget override, absolute GB (rtx6000 M3 partial-resident tier). Default is RESIDENT-IF-FITS (2026-08-02): exact expert-bank bytes summed from the GGUF header (per-layer x n_layer misprojects UD-quants both ways: Ornith-35B +7%, Qwen3.6-35B −2%) vs free VRAM minus the file's non-expert bytes minus the headroom reserve. Under PP, the expert numerator is partitioned by the actual stage-to-device placement; stages sharing one device are combined, while distinct devices decide from only their owned layer slices. The old 0.80 x free default reserved 20% of the card and spilled the Ornith-35B 19.5GB bank that fits: −33% decode / −54% prefill (research/residency-cap-20260802/) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_RESIDENT_HEADROOM_GB` | 2.0 | machine-specific headroom the resident decision reserves beside weights (CUDA ctx + KV + workspace; measured ~1.7GB at board shape on the 24GB 5090, serve c=8 peaked 23220/24463 MiB). Raise it on rigs co-running other GPU work or serving many long-ctx sessions |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PINNED` | pinned when MOE_CACHE on | force pinned host expert slabs |
| `MEMRA_MOE_SIZE_AWARE` | off | `1` partitions SLRU slots by expert projection size so mixed-size Hy3 tiers cannot strand capacity |
| `MEMRA_MOE_LFU` | off | `1` enables frequency-aware GPU SLRU eviction; this is separate from the fixed LRU policy in the optional CPU projection cache |
| `MEMRA_MOE_LFU_DECAY` | off | finite `(0,1]` epoch decay for GPU-cache frequency scores; `1.0` retains counts while still enabling forward-epoch accounting |
| `MEMRA_MOE_LFU_MTP_WEIGHT` | 1.0 | finite `0.25..=64` multiplier applied to GPU-cache frequency updates from MTP/verify epochs |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_DISK` | off | set = enable the NVMe disk tier for MoE experts (M3-class models that exceed host RAM) |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_PINNED_FRAC` | 0.60 | finite `(0,1]` fraction of `MemAvailable` the spill tier may pin. An out-of-range or non-numeric value warns once, uses the safe 0.60 default, and increments `config_fallbacks`; model load continues |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_IO` | `mmap` | expert storage backend: `mmap` (default and fallback), `pread` (blocking positioned-read oracle), `worker` (bounded CPU positioned-read prefetch), or `direct` (the same worker pipeline with O_DIRECT for 4 KiB-aligned expert extents). **Fail-closed (2026-08-13, hermes 8fca…): an unrecognized configured value REFUSES to start** (`configured_mode()` panics; unset still means `mmap`). Only runtime degradation is soft: unaligned direct extents and runtime backend/read failures fall back to mmap and increment the runtime `fallbacks`/error counters. Spill mode selects how expert bytes reach the GPU, so a silent downgrade hid a correctness-critical misconfiguration and let a spill benchmark report mmap numbers under a requested mode. |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_PREAD_DEPTH` | 2 | pinned-buffer count and worker-thread count for `worker` (`1..=64`; also sizes the blocking `pread` pool). An out-of-range or non-integer configured depth REFUSES to start (fail-closed since 2026-08-13); unset means depth 2. Hy3 on the local RTX 5090 measured depth 16 as the knee: it matched depth 32 at 1.18 tok/s after a reverse-order repeat, used 107MB rather than 214MB of pinned RAM, and removed depth-8 ring saturation (a typo'd depth had made a spill benchmark report default-depth numbers — hence the refuse). |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_WORKER_EXPERT_WINDOW` | `(pread_depth-1)/3`, minimum 1 | complete experts kept in the rolling positioned-read window; each expert occupies three projection buffers |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_LIB` | off | path to a trusted build of memra's optional Hy3-only native CPU-expert companion (ABI v2; no external inference runtime). `dlopen` executes constructors before ABI validation. Complete frozen-cache hits stay on CUDA; experts missing any projection run wholly on CPU |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_THREADS` | 8 | OpenMP compute threads for one-token CPU expert work (`1..=256`); the two-pass means for 8 and 12 threads differed by 0.7%, but the winner reversed by about 8% between individual reverse-order observations (100 timed calls each), so the lower-contention value remains the local default |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_IO_THREADS` | compute thread count | positioned-read workers used by the CPU expert backend (`1..=256`) |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_PIPELINE` | off | `1` overlaps companion reads with compute (cached experts compute immediately, missing experts in read-completion order, output byte-identical; the Hy3 launcher opts in). Requires `OMP_WAIT_POLICY=ACTIVE`: with passive waits the ready-batch region entries pay futex+C-state wakes and decode compute inflated 3.0→8.4 s per 32 tokens; with ACTIVE the spinners starve the caller between calls and e2e is flat vs serial (2026-07-22 bisect chain, N=3 4.55 vs N=5 control 4.50). Stays a door until the single-region structural fix removes the wait-policy dependence |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_IO_CPUSET` | inherit | optional cpu list (e.g. `8-15`) pinning the async read pool off the compute cores |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_EXECUTOR_CPUSETS` | inherit | `"0-7;8-15"` pins each CPU-expert executor to its own core group and sizes its OMP team to that group (`MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_EXECUTOR_THREADS="8;8"` overrides). Asymmetric partitioning for hybrid P/E silicon: one team per group, so a slow group never straggles a fast group's barrier. MEASURED +11% at m=3 mixed prompts (5.74/5.73/5.77 vs 5.30/4.94/5.27) and far more reproducible than the P-only baseline. Knees: 8 E-cores in a second group is the optimum (a 14-core E team falls back to baseline) and executors should be FEWER than in-flight calls, or the weakest group becomes the critical path. Requires GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY unset (a global affinity overrides per-thread pinning) and a process taskset covering the groups. Helps only when several expert calls are in flight — single-stream issues one at a time |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_CACHE_SHM` | off | `1` backs the expert cache with a named tmpfs segment and persists the index across restarts: a clean reopen starts warm (validated 100% hit / zero re-reads in the cross-process smoke). The object is created atomically with mode `0600`; an existing object is adopted only when `fstat` proves it is owned by the effective uid with mode exactly `0600`. Foreign or permissive objects are explicitly REFUSED and the process uses its private cache. Exclusive flock sends a second cooperating process to the same private fallback. Crash → dirty header → cold-but-correct start; cache keys pin device/inode/size/ctime, persisted rows are bounds-checked before pointer construction, and reload verifies a checksum over the whole entry up to 32 KiB or eight evenly spaced 4 KiB windows for larger entries. Costs THP on this rig (`shmem_enabled=never`) unless the sysctl is flipped to `advise` |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_CACHE_SHM_NAME` | `/memra-expert-cache-v1` | POSIX shm name for the persistent cache. A pre-existing name is usable only when its object is owned by the effective uid with mode exactly `0600`; unsafe adoption is refused rather than repaired |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_FREEZE_PROFILE` | off | path to a freeze-profile sidecar. A warmup run saves the frozen residency set (versioned header binds slot geometry); a later run restages it directly and SKIPS the profiling warmup. Measured pair (2026-07-22): 5285/5285 blocks restaged, startup wall 7:37.6→6:02.0 (−21%), identical 32 tokens, argmax MATCH, decode band unchanged. Mismatched/stale profiles fall back to a normal warmup; the post-freeze argmax gate still validates the assignment either way |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_CACHE_GB` | 16 | requested normal-RAM cache ceiling for CPU-routed expert projection bytes; the measured Hy3 5090 launcher requests 20 GiB and still applies the live-stack headroom gate below |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_RESERVE_GB` | 4 | live-stack RAM headroom retained by capping `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_CACHE_GB` to `MemAvailable - reserve` when the CPU companion initializes and prints the effective budget |
| CPU expert cache eviction | LRU | fixed winners-only policy; experimental LFU and layer-aware least-stale arms did not beat the measured LRU path and were removed |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_IO` | `buffered` | CPU expert reads: `buffered` or aligned `direct` positioned I/O |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_MIRROR_MAP` | off | tab-separated inode/alternate-path map for splitting large direct reads across byte-identical expert mirrors on two NVMe devices |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_FREEZE_CACHE` | off | `1` freezes HBM expert residency so CPU/GPU backend assignment cannot drift after warmup; required for exact repeatable Hy3 hybrid serving |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_FREEZE_WARMUP_TOKENS` | off | discarded tokens used to profile/fill HBM before freezing; the Hy3 5090 launcher uses 128 after one same-session, identical-token N=32 pair measured 4.52 tok/s versus 4.46 at 8 tokens (single pair, not a median), and `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_FREEZE_WARMUP_SPEC_K` selects speculative warmup depth in run-spec |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_FREEZE_PROFILE_ADMIT` | off | `1` lets CPU-routed warmup misses vote for HBM admission before the residency set freezes; current-token execution remains on CPU |
| `MEMRA_CPU_AFFINITY` | `0-7` in `run_hy3_local_5090.sh` | CPU list used by the Hy3 launcher for `taskset` and `GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY`; wrapper-only machine topology setting |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_BATCHED_PRIME` | off | rollback seam: `1` restores batched prompt replay after Hy3 CPU/GPU expert residency freezes; the local spill path otherwise replays tokenwise to avoid transiently rereading the missing expert bank |
| `MEMRA_ST_PINNED` | off | `1` pins the safetensors expert store — ONLY for fits-in-RAM checkpoints (pinning 26GB evicted the page cache: 30x regression, 2026-07-07) |
| `MEMRA_ST_REPACK_DISK` | on | `0` forces in-RAM gather instead of the `.memra-repack` disk cache (safetensors stream-repack loader) |
| `MEMRA_Q4F16` | capacity-keyed auto | dense-gemma (12B/31B geometry) f16 prefill mirrors on the cuBLASLt lane — the sm_120a sibling of the Hopper `MEMRA_PP_F16` class (`memra-engine/src/hybrid.rs`). With the flag unset, mirrors are admitted iff free VRAM covers the admissible f16 mass (every 2D Q8_0/Q4_0/Q6_K/Q4_K/Q5_K tensor `build_q8_f16` admits) + 8 GiB serving headroom (zoo-fusion arc, 2026-08-17: the 31B downQ6K trunk's Q6_K ffn_down otherwise rides the 3.46 ms/call dequant-GEMM prefill wall — 30% of c8 GPU time; mirrors measured c8 agg +37% / ttft −70%). `1` forces (still requires the f16 lane compiled in), `0` kills; 24 GB rigs refuse by construction |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_EXECUTORS` | 1 | CPU-expert executor count without explicit cpusets (`1..=8`; `memra-engine/src/cpu_experts.rs`). Ignored when `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_EXECUTOR_CPUSETS` is set — the group list then determines the count |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_PREFETCH_ANNEX_GB` | 1.5 | prefetch annex byte budget in the native companion (`tools/memra_cpu_experts.cpp`): LRU-held prefetched projection bytes awaiting promotion, clamped `0.125..=16.0` GiB. Companion-side (requires `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_LIB` + `MEMRA_MOE_PREFETCH=1`) |
| `MEMRA_CPU_EXPERT_PREFETCH_MAX_INFLIGHT` | 32 | max in-flight prefetch reads in the companion (`1..=4096`; out-of-range falls back to 32). Same companion scope as the annex budget |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PREFETCH_TOP` | 1 | how many predicted next experts the `MEMRA_MOE_PREFETCH=1` pipeline prefetches per layer (`1..=8`; `cpu_experts.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PREFETCH_MIN_LAYER` | 40 | first layer index eligible for the prediction-table prefetch (early layers churn; `cpu_experts.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_KQ_NVFP4` | 0 | load-time k-quant→NVFP4 re-encode: `1` = Q4_K, `2` = +Q5_K. +3.9% 9B plain but ~2x quant error and an acceptance tax — bpw equality ≠ quality-class equality (2026-07-08). Speed-mode opt-in only |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_F8F4` | off | `1` = W4A8-FP8 prefill MMQ (e4m3 fold + f8f6f4 MMA, 381-TF class). pp +3.9-6.3% ALL models, TTFT -4-5.6%; e2e spec MODEL-SIGNED via the prefill-KV acceptance law (27B ST +7.2%, 9B -3.5/-6.1%) — per-model serve adoption (NV-27B ST config), never a global default (2026-07-10 flip battery). **RE-VERDICT 2026-08-06 (`research/f8f4-flip-20260806/`): still NO global flip.** The route got faster — `7c0df07b`'s block_scale MMA form is 1.2153x (q27) / 1.1485x (q9) and **bit-exact** vs plain f8f4, so it cannot repair a route-level acceptance dip. Reachable on **2 of 12** gate models (the single `QT_NVFP4 && use_w4a8` dispatch arm); everything else is a structural no-op. Red cells: q27 bare-head K=3 (its serve K) −3.6pp deterministic 5/5, and q9 + its **production regime drafter** −9.5pp on a short prompt (OFF==OFF2 control green). All exactness gates PASS both arms (incl. chunk-invariance and serve-smoke/stress c=64) and greedy divergence lands *past* the 20-token goldens — the battery is blind to this class. The acceptance **sign follows (model × drafter × prompt)**, not the model: it inverted between the embedded MTP head and the regime drafter on the same day. Adopting the route on a new artifact requires its own receipt via `research/f8f4-flip-20260806/tools/regime_accept_ab.sh`. Expert-tile twin measured NEGATIVE (-6.4% best variant, not pipe-bound) and was deleted — tag moe-f8f4-negative |
| `MEMRA_NV_W4` | off | `1` re-quants F8 attention weights → NVFP4 on the NVIDIA-official 27B (+20% plain, acceptance held, 2026-07-07). Opt-in until the text battery proves the class |

For the official Step-3.7 FP8 checkpoint, `MEMRA_ST_E4M3` also selects the
expert numerical program at load time. Unset or `1` keeps the stacked expert
banks native and runs the dynamic-E4M3 W8A8 correctness reference. Literal
`0` materializes the historical Q8 diagnostic representation before serving.
That diagnostic process is never Step FP8 completion or performance evidence.

### Build-time (build.rs / nvcc)

| flag | default | what it does |
|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_NVCC` | `nvcc` on PATH | nvcc binary override |
| `MEMRA_CUDA_ARCH` | auto-detected | build target: `120a` (Blackwell consumer), `90a` (Hopper), `100a` (B200), `89` (portable eval). Unset = probe the GPU via `nvidia-smi compute_cap` (12.x→120a, 9.0→90a, 10.0→100a, 8.9→89); GPU-less/unknown falls back to `120a` so the CI compile gate is unchanged. Explicit value always wins. `89` builds the secondary correctness-first eval lane: native-FP4 prefill compiled off, tiled GDN on its generic fallback, prompt attention through f32 SDPA |
| `MEMRA_ARCH_CHECK` | on | `=0` skips the Engine::new device-vs-binary compute-capability guard (fatbins are single-arch SASS; the guard fails early with a rebuild hint on mismatch) |
| `MEMRA_CUTLASS` | off | set = compile the CUTLASS sm120 NVFP4 GEMM (`cutlass_smoke`, `MEMRA_FP4_CUTLASS` door) |
| `MEMRA_CUTLASS_ROOT` | flashinfer venv tree | CUTLASS header tree location |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_X_Q45K` | 64 | k-quant MMQ X-tile (compile-time sweep seam) |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_X_Q4` | 128 | q4_0 MMQ token-tile rebuild (`64\|96`; same axis as the q45k/w4a8 seams — build-time, the tile is a template constant) |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_X_FP8` | v2 default (X=256 wide candidate) | per-block FP8 prefill tile token-width rebuild — sets the WIDE candidate; the launcher picks between it and `FP8_MMQ_X_SMALL` per call by wave fill (`build.rs`; history in the `MEMRA_FP8_MMQ` §2 entry) |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_X_IQEXP` | 128 | expert-segmented IQ MMQ token-tile rebuild (round-45 kernel-rate dig: smaller tiles trade MMA j-reuse for CTAs/SM) |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_FOLD_CEILING` | off | **CEILING PROBE, NOT SHIPPABLE**: rebuilds the NVFP4 W4A8 MMQ with the per-k01 f32 scale-fold collapsed to an s32 add — output is NUMERICALLY WRONG by construction (exactness gates WILL fail, by design). Measures the upper bound of fold-removal levers only (`research/prefill-gemm-20260806/`) |
| `MEMRA_FA_PP_MINBLOCKS` | kernel default | build-time sweep of `fa_prefill_f32_pp`'s `__launch_bounds__` minBlocksPerMultiprocessor (tune seam) |
| `MEMRA_IQEXP_K16` | off | Rollback seam for the IQ-experts/IQ4_XS-dense MMQ tiles' MMA **depth** (`cu/mmq_iq_experts.cu`, 2026-08-07, lane/iq-experts-k32). Set `=1` = rebuild with the ORIGINAL two-`m16n8k16.s8` form per 32-value k step; unset (default) = ONE `m16n8k32.s8` — the int8 pipe is K-FREE on sm_120a (both 16.06 cyc/warp-MMA, so k32 = 2x MACs/instr) and the fold arity halves (1 C tile / 1 dA / 1 FMA vs 2/2/2). Legal because all three tile loaders replicate the per-32 scale into both per-16 x_df slots (`s>>1` indexing), so one k32 MMA sums both halves in the exact s32 accumulator under one shared scale. **NOT bit-identical to k16** (the PTX audit's "candidate bit-identical" missed the f32 fold-shape change: k16 rounds `C0*d + C1*d` separately, k32 rounds `(C0+C1)*d` once — prime logits differ ~80% of bytes, maxdiff 1.7e0/6.7e-1 gemma/KAT, argmax MATCH, top-10 sets near-identical); branch-(b) class, and the kernel was already gated vs dp4a on closeness+argmax+spec, never byte-identity. SASS: 1536x IMMA.16832 vs 3072x IMMA.16816 (exact 2:1). Gates on k32: kernel-check ALL GREEN model-backed (KAT rel<=2.7e-4, bar 1e-3), run-gen argmax MATCH (gemma26b + KAT), run-spec K=1..8 8/8 PASS (KAT + owntrim drafter). Perf N=5 interleaved adjacent-alternating pairs, one lock hold: gemma26b pp2311 **+5.15%** (6893.4 -> 7248.5, DISJOINT, 5/5), KAT pp2048 **+2.82%** (3903.6 -> 4013.5, DISJOINT, 5/5). Receipts: `research/iq-k32-20260807/` |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_X_W4A8` / `MEMRA_MMQ_Y_W4A8` | 128 / 128 | W4A8 MMQ tile sizes. Swept 2026-07-06: defaults optimal on the 5090 (X=32 −28%, Y=64 −2%); kept as autotuner levers for other silicon |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_FP8BLK_PLAIN` | off | Rollback seam for the per-block FP8 prefill tile's MMA **form** (`cu/mmq_fp8_blk.cu`, 2026-08-06, lane/rp-on-st). Set = issue plain `mma.sync.aligned.kind::f8f6f4.m16n8k32`; unset (default) = `kind::mxf8f6f4.block_scale.scale_vec::1X … ue8m0` with the **identity scale `0x7F7F7F7F`**, which computes the bit-identical e4m3×e4m3 product at **16.06 cyc/warp-MMA instead of 32.02** on sm_120a. Worth **+6.54% e2e pp512** on the 27B block-128 checkpoint (N=6 adjacent alternating pairs, DISJOINT, spread <0.09%); bounded below the 1.994x MMA-rate ratio because this tile measures 110–130 TF and was never MMA-bound. Exactness is not inherited: `fp8-mmq-check` reports **byte-for-byte identical** verdicts across the two forms (9 shapes × 2 arms, EXACT arm 0 bit-mismatch, 254/254 e4m3 codes) and live prefill logits over 1040 dispatches differ in **0 of 993280 bytes** (md5 equal, two passes). Same one-line change as `MEMRA_MMQ_F8F4_PLAIN` on the W4A8 tile. Receipts: `research/rp-on-st-20260806/` (`fp8blk-form-ab.*`, `pplogits-*`, `blksc-noreg.*`) |
| `ACCPROBE_F32_PLAIN` | off | **Research instrument only** — rebuilds the F32 arm of `accprobe-bench`'s kernel (`cu/mmq_q8_0_f32acc.cu`) on the plain `kind::f8f6f4` MMA form. Exists solely so the FP8-ST v3 gate's published Q1 numbers stay reproducible: that gate measured its F32 arm at 32.02 cyc/warp-MMA against an s32 control at 16.06 and attributed the whole delta to the **accumulator**, so its "+16.7/+23.1pp weighted → GO" was the MMA form. Equalized (the default now), the sign flips to **−6.7/−8.8pp** and the v3 case is refuted — see `research/fp8v3-gate-20260805/VERDICT.md`'s correction header and `research/rp-on-st-20260806/accprobe-*`. No dispatch seam; this TU is never linked into a serving path |
| `MEMRA_GEMM_K1_LAUNCH` | shipped (128,128,8) | `"BM,BN,NWARP"` kernel1 launch-tile override — MUST match the `-D K1_BM/K1_BN/NWARP` the swept fatbin was built with (tools/sweep) |

`MEMRA_*_FATBIN*` are **internal build plumbing** (build.rs → rustc-env fatbin paths consumed
by `include_bytes!(env!(...))`), never user flags. The exhaustive set (2026-08-19 audit):
`MEMRA_ENGINE_FATBIN`, `MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN` (+ its KV-format variants `MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN_KF8`,
`MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN_VF8`, `MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN_VQ4`, `MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN_KF8VF8`,
`MEMRA_FLASH_FATBIN_KF8VQ4`), `MEMRA_GEMM_FATBIN`, `MEMRA_QMATVEC_FATBIN`, `MEMRA_ROUTER_FATBIN`,
`MEMRA_SAMPLE_FATBIN` (spec_sample), `MEMRA_HYBRID_FATBIN`, and `MEMRA_FATBIN` (memra-probe's
own module). The same class covers `MEMRA_BUILD_SHA` (memra-server build.rs → the OpenAI-shape
`system_fingerprint`) and `MEMRA_BUILT_CUDA_ARCH` (memra-engine build.rs → the
`MEMRA_ARCH_CHECK` device-vs-binary guard's compiled-arch side). `MEMRA_KV_KFMT`/`MEMRA_KV_VFMT`
are the `-D` defines build.rs derives from `MEMRA_KV_K`/`MEMRA_KV_V` per flash fatbin.
`MEMRA_MMVQ_ROWS` is a compile-time `#define` in qmatvec.cu, not an env var.

---

## 3. Rollback seams (default ON — `=0`/set reverts to reference path)

These exist because correctness discipline needs a same-binary oracle. Each is a measured winner; the seam is the documented way back.

| flag | revert semantics | provenance |
|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_CKPT=0` | dspark round only (armed route): partial-accept restore back to the snapshot+replay oracle instead of the MTP column-stash direct restore | default-on in the dspark route 2026-08-18; column-stash cut restore 22ms/round -> 0.35ms, spec 23->38 tok/s at placeholder acceptance; `MEMRA_DSPARK_CKPT_GATE=1` byte-gates the two arms per partial round |
| `MEMRA_STATE_COPY_BATCH=0` | dspark verify round: per-layer GDN snap/commit state copies back to the eager per-buffer dispatch loop instead of the batched pointer-table kernel | default-on 2026-08-20 (engine-bundle slice 1); batching cut snap 0.63->0.19 ms/rd + commit 0.24->0.13 ms/rd, agentic gate +3.1%; launch-structure only, bytes unchanged (E2E-gated) |
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_FA_ROWS=0` | qwen35 t-parallel verify: full-attention per-row KV-append + fa-decode loop instead of the z-batched seqs twins (one append + one fa main + one combine per layer) | default-on 2026-08-20 (fa-execupdate slice 4); agentic gate 109.4->114.3 tok/s (-0.9 ms/rd), verify wall 18.51->17.64; straddle rounds fall back per row automatically; bytes unchanged (E2E + bank-matched) |
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_VG_AUTOFREE=1` | `MEMRA_DSPARK_VERIFY_GRAPH=1` graphs only: instantiate captured verify graphs with `AUTO_FREE_ON_LAUNCH` instead of `USE_NODE_PRIORITY` | PRIORITY default 2026-08-20 (fa-execupdate slice 4): the AUTO_FREE launch-time mem-pool scan cost 25.6 us x 16 segment launches ≈ 0.41 ms/rd; captured alloc nodes are balanced by in-graph frees so AUTO_FREE had nothing to reclaim |
| `MEMRA_FAST=0` | Stage-A f32-dequant matvec class — THE correctness oracle | default-on 2026-07-08 (env-law retirement) |
| `MEMRA_PDL=0` | plain `cuLaunchKernel` for ALL PDL-attributed kernels (master seam: the six glue kernels + every later wave) | default-on 2026-07-13; E4B +1.0-1.2%, 26B/31B/qwen flat; SASS-audited entry syncs |
| `MEMRA_PDL_MMVQ=0` | revert PDL wave-A alone: the four decode-hot mmvq matvec kernels (`q4_0_mmvq_rp`, `fused2_mr1_rp`, `fused3_mr1_rp`, `q6_K_mmvq`) | default-on 2026-07-23; 12B +0.19%, E4B A/B positive, 31B/26B flat |
| `MEMRA_PDL_WB=0` | revert PDL wave-B alone: dense-glue quartet (rms_norm_f32, add_rms_norm_f32, add_scale_rms_norm_q8_1, quantize_q8_1) + flash trio (append_kv dc, the two q8 rows-combines) | default-on 2026-07-23; B1a 12B +0.61% |
| `MEMRA_WPF=0` | no wo-plane L2 prefetch across the E4B fa window | default-on 2026-07-13; +0.65% E4B; 26B/31B probed flat/negative and NOT wired |
| `MEMRA_F2B=0` | separate per-tensor b-tier verify launches (no segmented-grid qkv/gate+up fusion) | default-on 2026-07-13; 31B depth +5%, short +3.9%, 26B +0.9%; bit-exact (VERIFY-GATE 0.000e0) |
| `MEMRA_Q8_FFN_FUSE2=0` | two `matmul_pre_noscale` launches for the Q8_0 dense-FFN gate+up pair at m=1 (no `fused2` dispatch in `matmul_pre_dual_noscale`) | default-on 2026-08-05 (q27 deep dive): the pair was 128 of 1015 launches/token on q27-Q8_0, the largest un-fused class; fusing = +0.94% (N=5, 5/5 pairs both orderings), BIT-IDENTICAL streams (kernel-check Q8-FUSED2 bits=true; body is `qmatvec_q8_0_mmvq` verbatim per (tensor,row)). Bails when a `MEMRA_Q8RP` split-plane mirror exists (fused2 has no `_rp` twin). Receipts `research/q27-deepdive-20260805/` |
| `MEMRA_MMVQ=0` | dp4a matvec class (m=1 AND batched verify switch together — dispatch-parity law) | default-on 2026-07-08; parity fix 2026-07-07 |
| `MEMRA_IQ_FAST=0` | non-expert IQ4_XS matmuls back to the Stage-A f32 oracle path | default-on 2026-08-02 (`research/kat-anomaly-20260802/`) — the old opt-in default WAS the KAT-Coder decode anomaly: its IQ4_XS trunk (attn_qkv/attn_gate/ssm_out/shexp, ~0.52GB/tick) rode the oracle kernel at m=1 AND prefill; dp4a admission moved decode 106.7 -> 193.4 (+81%, x5 interleaved) and pp512 228 -> 697. Dispatch-parity by construction (no mmvq/batched IQ4_XS kernels exist — every m rides the same dp4a program, verify == decode). Expert-bank IQ4_XS has its own dispatch; supported models are dispatch-unchanged, proven by the q35 ctrl bit-identity guard |
| `MEMRA_MOE_CACHE=0` | stage-every-token expert dispatch (no SLRU) | default-on 2026-07-08 |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PREFETCH=1` | pipeline the next routed expert's cache misses on the copy stream | experimental; target-rig gate required |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PAGE_PREFETCH=1` | issue rolling `MADV_WILLNEED` for mmap-backed GGUF/repack ranges | experimental; cold-cache rtx6000 + RTX 5090 A/B required |
| `MEMRA_MOE_PAGE_PREFETCH_WINDOW` | future experts kept in the rolling page-prefetch window (default `1`; `0` disables) | only read when `MEMRA_MOE_PAGE_PREFETCH=1`; tune to storage latency/page-cache budget |
| `MEMRA_MOE_MMAP_ADVICE` | whole-map expert advice: `random` (default) or `normal` | `normal` restores ordinary Linux readahead; invalid values warn and fall back to `random` |
| `MEMRA_NO_FA_VEC` (set) | scalar `fa_decode_f32` bit-reference (eager + rows + graph in lockstep) | vec default-on 2026-06-28 |
| `MEMRA_FA_V2=0` | per-key online-softmax FA twins (v2 = tile-batched, own numeric config) | default-on 2026-07-08, the depth-slope fix |
| `MEMRA_FA_ROWS_OFF=1` | per-row verify FA loop instead of the fused rows kernel | rows landed 2026-07-03 (+13.8% 9B p2) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_LEAN=0` | zeroed verify buffers + rows dispatch at t=1 | default-on 2026-07-08 (+1.5–2.4% 35B) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_M2=0` | per-m grid.y=m verify dispatch at t=2 (no small-m batched twin) | default-on 2026-07-09 (flattened the 35B verify K-curve; K=4 within 1–3% of K=3) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FUSED_T=0` | per-tensor decode-exact verify trunk calls (no t=2-4 launch fusion) | default-on 2026-07-09; bit-identical per (tensor,token,row) by construction (kernel-check Q8-FUSED2-B/FUSED3-B gates) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_DUAL_T=0` | two single launches for the NVFP4 verify FFN gate+up pair at t=2-4 (no dual twin) | default-on 2026-08-02 (+0.8% q27 spec e2e at K=3, -0.2..-0.3ms/pass at T=2-4 x9 interleaved pairs); bit-identical per (tensor,token,row) by construction (kernel-check DUAL-BATCHED gates, both layouts) |
| `MEMRA_NVFP4_AUX_DUAL=0` | two separate `ssm_beta`/`ssm_alpha` NVFP4 verify launches at t=3 | default-on 2026-08-11 for the 27B's 48-row split-plane pair: one 24-block WROWS=1 dual replaces two 12-block singles. Development rig (5090, 82 SM): +1.15% K=3 spec throughput (N=5 interleaved, 95.98 to 97.08 tok/s). Verification rig (Vast 2x PRO 6000 WS, 188 SM): FLAT (-0.0244% median, N=5 interleaved, mixed paired signs) with exactness green — the default rides bit identity plus a dev-rig win, NOT a transferred speedup. kernel-check `DUAL-BATCHED-AUX` bit identity both rigs; receipts `research/27btune-20260811/` + `research/vast27b-20260811/` |
| `MEMRA_B567=0` | b8-wide verify matvecs at T=5..7 (the pre-vt-fixes 8-wide register tax — the measured T=4→5 cliff) | default-on 2026-08-03 (lane/vt-fixes fix 1/1b: exact-width b5/b6/b7 + dual gate+up twins, NVFP4 split-plane trunk, rpsc+rpr2w8 schedules); BIT-IDENTICAL to the b8 launch by construction (columns c >= m never execute in either form; kernel-check m=7 row bit-bad=0). Part of the wave's +3.1-4.2% spec e2e (receipts `research/vt-fixes-20260803/`) |
| `MEMRA_TK_FA_GROUP=0` | qwen35 t-parallel full-attention q/k/v as three single batched launches | default-on 2026-08-20 (lane/dspark-trunk-kernels slice D): the group4 kernel with n3=0 computes wq/wk/wv (12288/1024/1024) in ONE grid — the two 1024-row singles were 9us 32-block launches. Bit-identical per (tensor,token,row) exactly as the group4 door; same eligibility mirror |
| `MEMRA_TK_GDN_GROUP=0` | qwen35 t-parallel GDN in-projection 4-tuple as four single batched launches | default-on 2026-08-20 (lane/dspark-trunk-kernels slice C): `qmatvec_nvfp4_mmvq_group4_b*_rp` computes wqkv/wqkv_gate/ssm_beta/ssm_alpha in ONE grid over the concatenated row space (nsys-B verify scope: the tuple was 160 launches/rd = 2.16 ms/rd, the two 48-row tensors 8.5us per 12-block launch). Per (tensor,token,row) the body is `nvfp4_mmvq_batched_rp` verbatim, macro-scale fused at the write == the conditional scale_inplace pass -> bit-identical to the four singles. Split-plane rp NVFP4 only, out_f%8==0 all members, m=2..16 (the b16 form serves m=9..16; `matmul_nvfp4_fused3/4` delegate that width class here — lane/orndecode2 — so the door sits on the exact-16 decode-batch trunk as well as t-parallel verify) |
| `MEMRA_TK_FFN_DUAL=0` | qwen35 t-parallel verify FFN as two singles + `silu_mul` + standalone quantize (the pre-slice-B chain) | default-on 2026-08-20 (lane/dspark-trunk-kernels slice B): the qwen35 body never called the PROVEN dual doors — gate+up now ride `matmul_decode_exact_dual_pre` + `silu_mul_scaled_q8_1` + `matmul_decode_exact_pre` (the q27 verify chain verbatim; each door bit-identical/value-exact per its own banked receipts). nsys-B verify scope: the pair was 107 single launches/rd at grid 4352 |
| `MEMRA_NORM_ILP=0` | v1 `rms_norm_f32`/`add_rms_norm_f32` (scalar strided element loop) | default-on 2026-08-20 (lane/dspark-trunk-kernels): `_v2` twins unroll the element loop 4-deep — the verify T-row norms (grid=T, block=256) were serial load->fma latency chains (nsys-B verify scope: 11.8-12.2us/inst, 130 inst/rd = 1.51 ms/rd). BIT-IDENTICAL per row: same per-thread element order into one accumulator (same fma chain), block reduce verbatim, epilogue expression unchanged — holds at every (ncols, blockDim) incl. the 1024-thread `rms_norm_decode` sites |
| `MEMRA_FA_V3=0` | FA v2 decode twins (v3 = dp4a-K hybrid, own numeric config — int8 Q quantization) | default-on 2026-07-09 after full battery green (kernel-check + run-gen MATCH 35B/9B + run-spec PASS + graph gate bit-identical); requires default q8_0/q5_1 KV + hd%128==0 (host-gated, auto-falls back to v2) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_REPLAY=1` | legacy rollback+replay partial accept (also the j==0 fallback). Only literal `1` enables this operator seam; unset, empty, `0`, and other values are off. Q35-MoE's architecture-local exactness replay is independent of this flag. | replay-free default 2026-07-03 (+10–32%). Current 5090 Q9 control: N=5 balanced, `=0` 226.43 tok/s vs unset 226.05 (+0.17%); legacy `=1` 146.88 tok/s. All arms keep acceptance 0.815 and the same token hash. See `research/spec-replay-flag-20260814/RESULTS.md`. |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_NOREFRESH=1` | chain-approximate draft-KV entries (no true-hidden refresh) | refresh default 2026-07-03 (+4–6% acc) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_NOGRAPH=1` | eager draft chain (no CUDA-graph draft) | graph draft 2026-07-03 |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_GATE=0` | serve: always-spec at every concurrency and placement (no admit gate, no demotion). **Not a numeric config**: greedy output is unchanged; only the scheduler phase changes. This is also the PP-2 rollback/crash-gate door now that the placement default is spec admission OFF. | gate default-on 2026-08-07 and placement-aware 2026-08-08. Single-card takes the measured spec/batched upper envelope (current q9: spec wins c=1/2, plain wins c=4); PP-2 defaults plain at every rung because both q9 and step35 lose at c=1/2/4. Demotion exactness: forced at generated=129, 3122 bytes / 768 tokens byte-identical to spec-off solo; accept-gate --full 6/6, run-spec K=1..8 PASS, serve-smoke 0 fail, serve-stress c=64 64/64, apikeys-gate 18/18. Receipts `research/spec-gate-20260806/`, `research/specplace-20260808/`. |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TOKENWISE=1` | force tokenwise decode-step prime in every phase (<16-tok prompts take it anyway). Frozen Hy3 CPU/GPU expert serving selects tokenwise automatically after its profiling warmup. Also the seam that pins the FIRST generated token to the tokenwise oracle stream on near-tie prompts — the batched prime is a different numeric config and legally flips ~7% of first tokens at margins <= 0.70 (gap #46, `research/prime-gate-coverage-20260802/`) | batched prime 2026-07-03 (23x TTFT at 6k) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_APPEND_LOOP=1` | per-row KV append instead of the batched `_rows` kernel | measured equal 2026-07-03 |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_DEQW=0` | inline-dequant prefill FA (no bf16 dequant-once workspace) | deqw default 2026-07-05 (32k prime 1.60x) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_GKV=0` | gemma GLOBAL (hd512) layers back to q8_0/q5_1 KV (default = e4m3 via the kf8vf8 module) | fp8-globals default-on 2026-07-11 — the depth-plain lever (dequant-latency-bound, ncu) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_WKV` | gemma WINDOWED (hd256) layers: e4m3 vs q8_0/q5_1 KV. DEFAULT IS SERVING-KEYED (2026-07-12): `MEMRA_DRAFT` set (spec serving) → q8/q5_1, else → e4m3. Explicit `=0`/`=1` always wins | fp8-windows win depth-plain (+3%, 2026-07-12 A/B) but GUT the MTP drafter's acceptance (31B short .758→1.000 on q8/q5 = spec 88→122.7, ABOVE llama-mtp 112; 26B depth .57→.89) — the drafter's single swa attention is fragile to e4m3 KV noise. Globals (`GKV`) cost no acceptance |

Purely a BANDWIDTH lever since 2026-08-06 (lane/rp-on-st): it used to double as the exact-16 serve tier's admission ticket on Q8_0 trunks, because Q8_0's b16 kernel existed only in `_rp` form — `qmatvec_q8_0_mmvq_b16` (base layout) removed that coupling, so the tier now admits Q8_0 mirror or no mirror (see `MEMRA_DECODE_BATCH_CAP`, §7). Default: plain serving ON everywhere; SPEC serving is per-model — big dense (n_embd≥3500) forces tiling, while every unqualified hardware class fails closed to the deterministic TILE form. The former per-process CUDA-event timing pick was acceptance-unsafe because SK and TILE produce different stable token programs. On the 82-SM RTX 5090, cold-sidecar interleaved N=5 measured TILE at 0.906 acceptance / 354.71 tok/s median and SK at 0.797 / 298.04 tok/s; every arm was 128/128 exact against its own plain program, and two unforced boots reproduced TILE. Explicit 0/1 controls entry; `MEMRA_MMQ_SK_FORM` pins the numerical form. A hardware-specific SK default requires its own exact interleaved gate. | sk entry default 2026-07-23; deterministic form 2026-08-14 |

| `MEMRA_PRIME_SLABS=0` | per-call transient allocations instead of the resident per-device prime slab pool (the eager prime's trunk transients; per-device keyed since lane/pp-leverb) | `hybrid_forward.rs` |
| `MEMRA_LOCKSTEP_GROUPED` | Hy3 multi-stream lockstep decode, MoE layers: `1` = force grouped (all m rows through `moe_ffn_lockstep`, resident experts amortize weight reads; CPU-assigned experts keep per-row companion calls), `0` = per-row, unset = AUTO grouped from m>=3 (M2 gate 2026-07-23: m=2 6.17 base vs 5.85 grouped; m=3 6.31 grouped) | `decode.rs` |
| `MEMRA_PP_PIPE` | W4A8 NVFP4 prefill MMQ smem schedule (CUDA-side `getenv`, `cu/mmq_nvfp4_w4a8.cu` — predates and is UNRELATED to the ppN `MEMRA_PP_*` family): `1` = cp.async multi-stage pipeline, DEFAULT since 2026-07-09, bit-identical (data movement only); `0` = the unpipelined tile; `2` = the w4a8v2 2-CTA/SM occupancy mode, default OFF (single y slot, no ring; the 64x64-tile probe was net flat) | 2026-07-09 |

## 4. Diagnostics & test config

| flag | what it does |
|---|---|
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_CKPT_GATE=1` | dspark route only: run BOTH partial-accept restore arms (column-stash + snapshot-replay oracle) per partial round and byte-compare the resulting cache state — the same-binary exactness gate for `MEMRA_DSPARK_CKPT` |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_ANATOMY=1` | eager MTP draft-step phase timers (glue/attn/ffn/head, cumulative stderr summary every 128 steps). Phase boundaries sync the stream so absolute time inflates — the BREAKDOWN is the signal. Diagnostic only (lane/draftcost-moe, 2026-08-20; row backfilled by lane/dspark-sampled-admission after the flags census flagged the drift) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_STATS=1` | per-layer expert-cache hit/miss/staged-bytes prints (forces the stats-visible dispatch path) |
| `MEMRA_MOESD_DEPTH=<n>` | `target_efficiency` harness only (moesd dual-device sweep, `research/moesd-harness-20260811/`): prime_cache token depth for the target-forward matrix; default 128, floor 16 |
| `MEMRA_MOESD_CACHE_CAP=<n>` | `target_efficiency` harness only: expert-cache entry cap for the moesd target-forward sweep; refuses caps below the matrix's required residency |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_STATS=1` | print cumulative positioned-read snapshots when server requests finish: reads, logical bytes, errors, short reads, config-parse fallbacks (`config_fallbacks`), runtime mmap fallbacks (`fallbacks`), buffer waits, and worker ring-full events. Snapshots are totals, not per-request deltas; do not sum them |
| `MEMRA_MOE_TRACE=<path>` | append (layer, step, expert ids) per decode step — routing-locality analysis (`research/scripts/moe_trace_analyze.py`, 2026-07-07 M3 measurement) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_WEIGHT_TRACE=<path>` | append the PREFILL router's per-(layer, token) selected experts + weights — the routing-divergence oracle behind the m-invariance razor (`concat_prime_probe`, `research/concat-prime-exact-20260802/`). Forces the stats-visible dispatch path; diagnosis only |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_LOGIT_TRACE=<path>` | write one bit-preserving real decode router-logit row per layer, with correction bias, original-id active mask, scaling, and normalization metadata. The explicit diagnostic performs a DtoH readback and is not a performance mode |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_REPLAY=<path>` | `kernel-check` input for the served-logit host-vs-device replay cell; malformed, duplicate-layer, selected-id, or routing-weight-bit disagreement fails the required manifest gate |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_DISPATCH_TRACE=1` | print every engagement of Step-3.7's resident zero-DtoH sigmoid dispatch arm, including layer, token count, clamp form, and gate/up interleave state |
| `MEMRA_DUMP_HN=<path>` | append pre-head hidden states per decode step (head-MIPS feasibility probe, offline bound analysis; both decode arms share the door so they stay observably identical). Diagnostic only |
| `MEMRA_F16G_DEBUG=1` | grouped f16 expert lane stage bisect: full NaN/Inf scans of w/act/y plus post-permute/post-scatter bad-value counts — localizes a corrupt stage. Adds D2H syncs; diagnosis only |
| `MEMRA_FA_V4_MAX=1` | force the v4 FA lane at every t_kv (bypass the crossover) — correctness-forcing knob for the fp8 lane matrix (2026-07-12 closure battery) |
| `MEMRA_DRAFT_GRAPH_CHECK=1` | re-run the gemma draft chain eagerly after each graph replay and diff the drafted slots (non-destructive replay-vs-eager bisect) |
| `MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH_GATE=N` (gemma-gate) | E4B graph-door stream gate: `generate()` door OFF then ON on fresh caches, streams must be identical (the warmup-side-effect + exec-update oracle) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_GRAPH=1` | 12B/31B whole-token alloc-free graph door (slotted decode step, captured once, replayed per token). Bit-identical (96/96 stream gate) but −5.6% vs dc-eager on the 12B (campaign closed 2026-07-23: ladder 81.3→84.8 vs eager 89.8; residual is GPU-side graph-exec scheduling, not allocs/launch cost). Experimental door, default OFF |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_GATE=N` (gemma-gate) | 12B/31B graph-door stream gate: eager vs graph token streams over N tokens must be identical + both throughputs printed |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_DRAIN=N` | raise the graph door's drain window above the pinned 1 (EXPERIMENTS ONLY: relaunching the same graph exec before its prior launch completes is ILLEGAL — chunk=4 reproduced ILLEGAL_ADDRESS) |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_CENSUS=1` | print the captured graph's node-type census (kernel/memset/memcpy/alloc/free counts) at instantiate |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_CAPTIME=1` | phase-resolved capture cost per capture: `warmup2x / capture-body / instantiate / upload`. The split that killed the Q1 mem-node refactor — only instantiate+upload scale with node count (q27: 4.4 of 34.4 ms), the warmups are eager step executions and are node-count-invariant (`research/graph-allocfree-20260805/`) |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_IFLAG={priority,upload}` | re-instantiate the generic captured decode graph under a non-`AUTO_FREE_ON_LAUNCH` flag. Measures the auto-free launch-time mem-pool scan in isolation; measured FLAT on both probe and serve c=1 paths (q27 167.94 vs 167.65 tok/s, N=5 interleaved), so the default stays AUTO_FREE. `upload` is rejected by the driver here — cudarc's `end_capture` instantiates without an upload stream (`CUDA_ERROR_INVALID_VALUE`) |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_WARMUPS=N` | eager warmup runs before `capture_graph` captures (DEFAULT 1 since lane/graph-warmups 2026-08-05; min 1; `=2` = the rollback seam). The win: recapture −38% q27 / −41% q9 (pod) re-minted −42% q27 / −41% q9 on the 5090, decode +~1% both, capture+prime −13ms. Warmup 2's address-stability job (warmup 1 may grow/map the async pool; a stale capture reproduces the #68 baked-address class) is guarded structurally — balanced in-graph alloc/free pairs, retire-on-grow pools, capture-retain on the draft path — and was arbitrated adversarially: `tools/graph-warmup-stress-gate.sh` (pool-growth cycles both directions x10 + overlap arm + forced recaptures over freed blocks, bit-identity vs eager, canary teeth) GREEN at `=1` on the deployment rig. Receipts: `research/graph-warmups-5090-20260805/`, pod `research/graph-allocfree-20260805/logs/warmup-lever-N5.txt` |
| `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE2=K` (gemma-gate) | CHAINED batched-verify oracle: prefix tokenwise, then two back-to-back `decode_step_t` calls — per-position argmax must match the tokenwise chain. `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE2_DEV=1` runs the device-token verify arm (the spec round's exact path) |
| `MEMRA_ROUND_GRAPH_CHECK=1` | round-graph bisect: run the captured round body EAGERLY (no capture/replay) — splits body-semantics bugs from replay-mechanics bugs |
| `MEMRA_E4B_DCG_EAGER` | E4B door bisect arm: run the dcg step eagerly per token instead of capture/replay — `1` = exact live bucket, `2` = the capture's win bucket (separates bucket-path numerics from the replay mechanism) |
| `MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH_TIMING=1` | per-phase host timing of the E4B graph loop (dtoh-wait / fa_apply / launch sums) |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_NODES_DUMP=1` | dump the captured E4B graph's kernel-node inventory (symbol + grid + count) + fa update-unit count at capture |
| `MEMRA_BURST_VCHECK=1` | run the gemma verify-STREAM on each eager round's batch before the eager verify and diff argmaxes + per-layer KV byte sums (the burst verify's in-place gate harness) |
| `MEMRA_GATE_SEED=<n>` | decode-batch-gate synthetic-prompt token-pattern offset (default 0 = the historic prompt). Cross-config drift is near-tie roulette on any single synthetic prompt, so calibration re-sweeps draw several seeds — gate1-config sweeps 6 and FAILs iff >= 4 of the 6 draws diverge before step 3 (the fraction rule; 2026-08-02 5090 recal, `research/gate1-recal-20260802/`). The per-draw step floor of the round-45 H100 rule ("any seed < step 3"; tie flips assumed 6+) did not transfer: 5090 dice land at steps 0/1/3/4, proven dice by strict-equalized bit-identity on the same draws. Plumbing (wrong token/KV) diverges at step 0-2 on EVERY draw — the fraction separates at margin 2 on both sides |
| `MEMRA_GATE_CANARY=1` | decode-batch-gate TEST-ONLY plumbing canary: feeds gate1's batched lane one wrong token at step 1 — every draw diverges early and the fraction rule MUST FAIL (the teeth check for gate recalibrations; also trips strict bit-identity, as a real plumbing bug would) |
| `MEMRA_EXACT16_WHY=1` | print WHY a checkpoint is refused the exact-16 serve tier: `decode_batch_exact16_ok` is an ALL over every matmul in the model, so one class without a bit-exact b16 kernel refuses the whole checkpoint back to chunk 8, silently. Prints `[exact16] REFUSED by L<n>.<tensor> qtype=<q> rp4=<bool>` per refusing tensor (plus architecture / MLA-mixer / MoE-ffn refusals, which are structural and can never be admitted). Default off, no cost when off. Added lane/rp-on-st 2026-08-06, where it found FOUR blocking classes one at a time on real MIXED checkpoints — NVFP4, Q8_0-base, Q4_K, Q5_K — that had kept chunk 16 unreachable for every shipped artifact; without it each "still refused" round was a guess (`research/rp-on-st-20260806/`) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_GATE=0` | skip run-gen's batched-prime gate (#46): the last-position logits of `prime_cache` (the config that seeds real generation and serving) vs the tokenwise reference. A near-tie argmax flip is REPORTED non-fatally (the accepted cross-config drift class — measured 10/144 prompts across the six-model 2026-08-02 sweep, all at tokenwise margins <= 0.70; `research/prime-gate-coverage-20260802/`); a wide-margin flip or out-of-bounds drift FAILs. Dedicated multi-prompt battery: `prime-gate <model> --prompts-file f [--chat] [--strict]` |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_GATE_MAXDIFF` / `MEMRA_PRIME_GATE_MARGIN` | #46 verdict bounds (defaults 8.0 / 1.0, calibrated on the 2026-08-02 supported-set sweep: legal maxdiff up to 5.5 on gemma Q4_0, legal flip margins <= 0.70). Recalibrate when the kernels under them move — the H100 stale-verdict law |
| `MEMRA_TICK_TRACE=1` | memra-server: per-tick phase timing of the batched scheduler to stderr (diagnosis only) |
| `MEMRA_TTFT_TRACE=1` | memra-server: one `[ttft]` line per completion request with request-parse, HTTP admission, worker queue wait, tokenization, pre-prime setup, prime, first-decode wait, first serialized SSE body byte, cumulative boundary, and total timings. Debug-only; disabled requests allocate no trace state |
| `MEMRA_CONFIDENCE_TRACE=<path>` | memra-server: per-token confidence JSONL (`memra-token-confidence-v1`: reference logprob, top1 margin, entropy). Forces single-session serving and disables KV reuse + spec — a measurement harness mode, never a serving config |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_STATS=1` | per-slot accept histogram + draft-length histogram |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_DEMOTE_AT=N` | **test door, NEVER set in a deployment**: force the spec→batched demotion handoff when a session reaches N generated tokens, regardless of active-session count, and demote ONLY that session. Exists because the shipped trigger (`active >= MEMRA_SPEC_GATE_HIGH`) cannot be used for an exactness test — load-triggered demotion is nondeterministic in BOTH arrival timing and batch composition, and batched decode is not bit-identical across batch shapes (`fa_decode_batch_seqs_v4` carries one `split_keys` for sessions at different depths, and the batched-linear tier changes with B — pre-existing, found by lane/spec-gate: a spec-OFF discriminator arm diverges from its own solo reference at bytes 2379/1347 on two runs with no gate code involved). The door pins B=1 with no load, which makes the handoff itself — pending flush, `(cache, next_pred)` transfer, position, stream continuation — the only variable. Used by `research/spec-gate-20260806/exactness.py` (arm DEMOTE_SOLO vs REF); logs `FORCED at DEMOTE_AT=N (test door)` so a forced demotion can never be misread as a policy one |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE=1` | per-round wall decomposition of the spec loop (draft / verify / accept+commit) — no tracing, no extra syncs (each phase is naturally sync-bounded); printed once at loop end via spec-stats. Built by the verify-economics lane (2026-08-02, `research/verify-economics-20260802/`) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_SETUP_TRACE=1` | per-call wall decomposition of the serve spec burst's SETUP + TAIL segments (the round loop's internals are `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE`'s job) — pins the per-burst fixed cost (`research/spec-serving-20260801/`) |
| `MEMRA_ECON_N` / `MEMRA_ECON_TMAX` / `MEMRA_ECON_ONLY` | `spec-econ` probe-bin controls (verify-economics receipts): iterations per arm (default 50), max verify T (default 6), single-arm nsys attribution mode (`decode_h`/`verify_tN`) |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_SPEC=1` | per-round spec decode trace |
| `MEMRA_FILTER_COOP` | on | `0` = single-block `filter_stats` form (one block per row) instead of the cooperative multi-block kernel (`filter_stats_coop_f32`: 16 blocks/row, grid-synced bisection totals — lane/samplat 2026-08-21; the one-block form left ~94% of the device idle ~620us per B=8 serve tick). Same algorithm; slice-partial f32 sums are the accepted device-sampling class (sample-check arbitrates). Deterministic keying (hermes sweep 2026-08-23): the old per-call occupancy fallback (16*nrow <= SM count, else single-block) was a second numeric program selected by batch width — measured non-bit-identical (~1e-7..1e-6 rel renorm mass). Coop now engages on every >=16-SM device and CHUNKS rows to the co-residency cap (SM/16 rows per cooperative launch); per-row bits are batch-width-independent (kernel-check FILTER-COOP-CHUNK, bit-bad=0). `0` (or a <16-SM device class) is the only path to the single-block form — deployment-keyed, never per call; its distance is pinned by FILTER-COOP-VS-PLAIN (<1e-5) |
| `MEMRA_NVFP4_FUSED3B` | on | `0` = the attention q/k/v trio leaves the batched fused NVFP4 tier at m=2..8 (fused3-b2..b8, lane/samplat 2026-08-21) and runs as singles; m=9..=16 rides the group3 delegate regardless (its kill switch is `MEMRA_TK_FA_GROUP`) — rollback seam for the batched-tick decode increment |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_PRIMESEG=1` | memra-server: prints the plain-tier prime CALL SEQUENCE per request — each prime segment's boundary/stop and whether the W1 tokenwise door engaged (lane/spec-longctx-20260821; the diagnostic that made the sub-floor-remainder path loud). Diagnostic only, no numeric effect |
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_FULLG_DEBUG=1` | dspark verify graphs (slice 4c): per-round print of why the full-verify graph did or did not capture/replay (rung, coverage, batchable checks) — the door that diagnosed the MTP-slot walk-coverage bug (`f4da30f3d6`). Diagnostic only |
| `MEMRA_SKEY_PROBE=1` | sampled-draft-graph key probe (lane/graph-s-key-exactness-20260819): prints the burst-entry sampler facts (`[skey] burst sampled/pure_temp/top_k/top_p … graph_s_parked`) and flags `[skey] EXACTNESS q=0` when a drafted token gathers a zero filtered-q — the impossible-if-sound signature that confirmed the pre-fix key collision. Diagnostic only; no numeric effect |
| `MEMRA_MOE_CSR` | `0` = rollback the CSR expert-dedup gate_up on spec verify (default ON 2026-07-10: owner-scan dedup of the 38-40% duplicated expert weight-stream+decode, +1-2% spec e2e all K); `2` = run BOTH paths + byte-compare (debug) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_OVERLAP` | `1` = log cross-token expert-activation overlap at spec verify (unique/pairs ratio, diagnostic) |
| `MEMRA_FA_V4` | `0` = rollback to the v3 FA lane. DEFAULT ON 2026-07-10: key-per-lane score phase (zero shuffles/key), kernel -7.5..-15% at depth, e2e p2 +5.7% (35B), p3 +0.7-3.9% (all models), p1 -0.8% (noise); full battery green on all three models incl sampled seeded identity + kernel-check; acceptance deltas trajectory-class (±). `noB3`/`stage` = phase probes (WRONG OUTPUT, bench only) |
| `MEMRA_FA_DEEP` | `0` = rollback to the v4 twins from the deep twins (`fa_decode_vec_q_v4_deep`/`_deep_dc`). DEFAULT ON 2026-08-02 (lane fa-decode-deep): BIT-IDENTICAL rewrite of the v4 program — padded smem rows kill the 32-way score-phase bank conflict (1.40M → 3.5K conflict cycles/launch at d6144), packed 16B staging stores, L2 tile prefetch. NOT a numeric config: kernel-check pins deep-vs-v4 BYTE identity (2 geometries × 6 depths × eager/dc/bucketed-replay). Vec kernel d6144 29.7 → 20.8 µs (1.43x, quiet nsys); flat-or-better at every depth 96..6144 (`research/fa-decode-deep-20260802/`) |
| `MEMRA_FA_DEEP_MIN` | engagement floor for the deep twins (default 0 = always where v4 ran — the swept verdict, no losing cell on the fine grid). Sweep/diagnostic seam only; not a tuning knob |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_DEVACC` | `1` = device-side accept walk + seed gather + KV/recur rollback (round-stream stages a/b; token-identical, perf-neutral machinery — the stage-c consumer measured negative) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PIPE` | `1` = experimental reduced-matrix PP-2 two-session speculative pipeline (warm greedy unconstrained continuations only; requires `MEMRA_SPEC_DEVACC=1`). The merged TX-ticket seam is `verify_stage0_issue(...) -> VerifyBoundaryTicket -> verify_stage1_finish(...)`: stage 0 issues the boundary TX and returns the persistent slot, then stage 1 consumes that ticket through RX and publication. `MEMRA_SPEC_PIPE_TRACE=1` emits the timeline markers. The ordinary serial path calls stage 0 and stage 1 back-to-back with zero added CUDA callback, event, synchronization, or coordinator wait; supported shapes interleave A-stage1 with B-stage0, while unsupported shapes fall back to the ordinary serial burst. Box1 receipt: **+13.94% over serial** and **-47.89% vs plain**; experimental default OFF. Increment-1 state-fork recommendation is GO, bounded to fork + reconcile. Receipt: `research/opti0-20260810/RESULTS.md` |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_STREAM` | `1` = pre-issued M-round zero-readback bursts (round-stream stage c, `MEMRA_SPEC_STREAM_M` rounds/burst default 4). MEASURED NEGATIVE 2026-07-10 (35B serve p2 -16%, p1 -4%; 27B p2 wash): always-K draft + fixed-width verify waste > the ~1.5-2ms/round trip savings at real acceptance rates. Kept as the experimental seam. Token-identity is NOT universal: step35 has no ROUND-STREAM verify arm (hard refuse, spec.rs), and the qwen35 rowwise verify (t>16 or `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_ROWWISE=1`) refuses the stream too — only the paths that accept the burst carry identity |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_GRAPH` | **DEFAULT ON for the GatedDeltaNet + routed-MoE family** since 2026-08-23 (`=0` = kill switch, eager walk, byte-identical; `=1` opts other families in). Arms the slice-4c verify capture for the MTP spec round — the door already existed inside `qwen35_verify_tparallel` and had no caller on this route. WHY a default: this route reads verify-ISSUE 44-58% with verify-WAIT **0.0%** (`MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE=1`), i.e. host-launch-bound, and replay collapses that phase (55-62 -> 8-10 ms/burst). MEASURED on a non-serving current-generation host (9950X), balanced 4+4 boots both orders: OFF 266.0-266.5, ON 318.8-319.5 tok/s = **+19.7%**, no overlap, sub-1% per-arm spread, per-round 6.9 -> 5.7 ms; Zen 3 host +3-9%. The ON arm sits at ~320 on BOTH host generations while OFF tracks host speed — the arm moves the round off the host onto the device. EXACT: fixed-seed sampled hash identical ON/OFF and across hosts (`08941d5bb9762b21`), greedy seed-pinned likewise, run-spec K=1..8 PASS both arms with identical acceptance, kernel-check ALL GREEN. Default verified as a default (naked env -> pool ENGAGED + 319; `=0` -> pool absent + 266, twice each). SCOPE is the measured family only: Qwen3.8-27B is GDN + DENSE mlp and opts in with `=1` once it has its own interleave. Never co-armed with ROUND-STREAM; a round wider than the pool declines it. Receipts: `research/orndecode-20260822/VGRAPH.md` |
| `MEMRA_ROUTER_KERNEL` | `0` = rollback to the cuBLAS router (per-column gemv at verify t, gemvx at t=1). Default ON since 2026-07-10, extended to t=1 the same day: decode + verify route through the SAME kernel (parity by construction — the cuBLASLt n-dependence class is structurally gone). Verify +2-4% spec e2e; t=1 e2e wash (kept for parity) |
| `MEMRA_ROUTER_PREFILL_EXACT` | `0` = rollback to the batched cuBLASLt GEMMs for the MoE **prefill** router (`ffn_gate_inp`) and the shared-expert gate dot (`ffn_gate_inp_shexp`). Default ON 2026-08-02 (`lane/concat-prime-exact`): both cuBLASLt forms are **m-DEPENDENT** — a row's output changes when other rows join the call (router rows [0,19) move 3.9e-3 between m=19 and m=65; shexp gate 1.07e-4 between m=74 and m=75), and under cross-request concat prime batching that made a served request's own expert selection a function of its CO-ARRIVALS (16% of (layer,token) pairs got different experts). The in-house `router_gemv` / `sigmoid_dot_rows` twins — the ones decode and spec verify already use — are m-INVARIANT, so prefill rides them at every t. Fixes serve greedy c=1-vs-c=16 byte identity: Ornith-35B 6/16 -> **16/16**, KAT 7/16 -> **16/16** (Ornith-9B + Qwen3.6-35B ctrl stay 16/16). Receipts: `research/concat-prime-exact-20260802/`. The `=0` arm is a numeric A/B seam, NOT a serving configuration — it forfeits the isolation guarantee |
| `MEMRA_PROFILE_GEN` | `1` = cudaProfiler{Start,Stop} brackets run-gen's timed generate_with (prime included); `2` = capture starts at the DECODE LOOP (prime excluded) — pair with `nsys -c cudaProfilerApi`. Window-cutting whole-run captures misattributes the argmax-gate loop into decode shares (2026-07-10) |
| `MEMRA_PROFILE_SPEC` | `1` = cudaProfiler{Start,Stop} brackets generate_spec (prime included); `2` = capture starts at the ROUND LOOP (prime excluded) — pair with `nsys -c cudaProfilerApi`. Built 2026-07-10 after phase-isolation-by-subtraction proved unworkable on MoE (primes are not fungible: the first cold-stages the expert cache) |
| `MEMRA_LAYER_PROBE=1` | sync+print after every forward stage — bisects an in-graph ILLEGAL_ADDRESS (M3 bring-up tool) |
| `MEMRA_GDN_DIFF=1` | dual-run oracle: chunked GDN prefill checked against the sequential scan per call |
| `MEMRA_MOE_GATE=1` | byte-identity oracle: grouped-vs-sequential MoE FFN compare (pair contract; known benign q8-quantize diff class documented at the gate site) |
| `MEMRA_MMVQ_BV=<variant>` | force ONE NVFP4 batched/mmvq variant everywhere (base/pf/r2/r2w8/pfr2/ca/car2/rp*) — the per-variant measurement seam (auto = wave-aware winners; forced-BV concluded auto-optimal 2026-07-06) |
| `MEMRA_KQ_BV=<variant>` | same, narrowed to k-quant dispatch only (interleaved k-quant A/B without touching NVFP4) |
| `MEMRA_FA_FLOOR=1` | prefill-FA floor kernel variant (fa_hd128_check gate bin) |
| `MEMRA_MSCALE_PROFILE` / `MEMRA_MSCALE_NOEAGER` / `MEMRA_MSCALE_INTERLEAVE` | verify-mscale probe bin controls (`INTERLEAVE=1` alternates the arms) |
| `MEMRA_TEST_MODEL`, `MEMRA_LLAMA_TOKENIZE`, `MEMRA_ST_TEST_DIR` | test-suite input paths (tokenizer parity, safetensors header tests) |
| `MEMRA_GATE_DUMP_TOKENS=1` | test-only `gemma-gate` token-stream dump used by exact ON/OFF benchmark harnesses; does not alter inference |
| `MEMRA_KC_MODELS_DIR` | model dir for kernel-check's REAL-WEIGHT sections (the router weight-oracle bit-identity + m-invariance sweep added by `lane/fast-router`; battery scripts set it, unset skips with a note) |
| `MEMRA_KC_FAST=1` / `MEMRA_KC_ONLY=a,b,...` | kernel-check dev-loop scoping (fast-gate tier 0, 2026-08-02): `FAST` skips ALL weight-oracle sections (synthetic arms only, ~2s vs ~4.5min); `ONLY` runs sections whose name contains a csv term. Every skip prints LOUD; the full battery (no env) still gates merges/tags — see docs/TESTING.md |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_SK_FORM=sk\|tile` | pin the stream-k or xy-tile numerical form for measurement/rollback, bypassing the device-keyed default |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TOK_PER_MS` | dark-prefill chunk estimator: prime rate (tok/ms, default 8.0) used to size within-SLO-headroom prefill chunks in the x-lane QoS tick |

### Diagnostics backfill (2026-08-19 audit — all internal, none are operator knobs)

| flag | what it does |
|---|---|
| `MEMRA_DEBUG=1` | one-shot ENGAGEMENT receipts for fused/batched dispatch arms (`[memra] ... ENGAGED` to stderr) — the first dead-arm A/B lesson: a silent gate no-op'd a whole experiment, so the arm proves it is live in the log (`lib.rs`, `spec.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_AFFINITY=1` | memra-server: log WHY a plain-affinity checkpoint capture was skipped (the session then re-primes in full next turn) — `worker.rs` |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_FAPOOL=1` | fa partials-pool tracing (`[fa-pool] REALLOC/retire` lines) — the instrument for the #68 dangling-pool class (`lib.rs`, 7 sites) |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_ZERO_ALLOCS=1` | memset EVERY engine allocation — the global uninit-read discriminator (task #14 defect hunt; the prime-fn-scoped zeroing experiment could not cover engine-internal buffers). Massive launch overhead; debug only (`lib.rs alloc_uninit`) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TRACE=<path>` | chunk-invariance bisect seam: one JSONL row per (chunk, layer) with a hash of that layer's last-row post-residual hidden. Forces a dtoh + host hash per layer — never on in a measured or gated run (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_ANATOMY=1` | per-stage prime wall breakdown (attn_full / gdn_linear / moe / dense / norms): syncs the stream around each `prime_layers`/`forward_last` stage and prints cumulative ms — attribution shares only, the syncs serialize overlap (lane/moebatch-q35moe; the receipt that found MoE at 88.6% of the ornith15 14.7k prime) (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_DBB_SAMP=greedy\|temp\|filt` | decode-batch-bench serve-tick meta isolator: runs the batched step through `decode_step_batch_sampled_lean_masked` with that device-sample meta on every row — the instrument that split the filtered-sampling serve wall from the engine tick (`decode_batch_bench.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_G4_PRIME_TRACE=1` | gemma4 prime bring-up trace: layer-0 stage stats (NaN count + amax of q/attn/moe stages) to stderr (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_INPUT_TRACE_DIR=<dir>` | append per-layer MoE FFN input rows (t x n_embd f32) — forces the stats-visible dispatch path plus a dtoh per layer; diagnosis only (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_PROBE=1` | gemma bring-up bisect: per-layer trunk stats (host rms + max of the LAST token row) vs llama-eval-callback node stats (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_VISION_DEBUG=<dir>` | vision-tower stage dumps (`rust_<tag>.bin`, f32 LE) for the HF parity bisect — qwen (`vision.rs`) and gemma (`vision_gemma.rs`) towers; `gemma-vision-oracle` sets it to its own out dir |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_SK_DEBUG=1` | per-call `[mmq-sk]` dispatch print (in_f/out_f/m/nsm, env-vs-device source, SK\|TILE pick) from the q4_0 MMQ launcher — CUDA-side `getenv` (`cu/mmq_q4_0.cu`) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PP_ANATOMY=1` | one-shot PP-2 spec-verify boundary pricing to the server log (reverse hop / stage 0 / TX / stage 1 + head) without nsys. N=2 stages ONLY — the report names exactly two stages and must never imply it measured middle ones (`spec.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_REPLAY_NLL=1` | replay-acceptance's trunk arm also accumulates mean teacher-forced NLL over the forced tape (`spec.rs replay_acceptance`) |
| `MEMRA_GV_FORCE_CAUSAL=1` | **WRONG-ARM probe seam**: splice the vision overlay rows but keep the plain causal mask — exists only so the decisive probe can show the island mask itself changes the answer. Never on in serving (`hybrid_forward.rs`) |
| `MEMRA_GEMV_JSONL=<path>` | gemv-e4m3-bench: append one JSONL row per measured cell |
| `MEMRA_GS_PROF=1` | graph-session-gate: decompose the replay step (fa_apply vs launch+sync vs token D2H) on a fresh session |

### Merged-lane diagnostics backfill (2026-08-22)

| flag | default | what it does |
|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_CHAIN_PHASE` | off | `1` alternates token-chain work across phase streams without the cross-token event edges. This is a timing-ceiling probe with deliberately racing/wrong output; never use its tape for correctness or serving. |
| `MEMRA_DOWN_X4` / `MEMRA_MMV_BLOCK` | off / 128 | `DOWN_X4=1` assigns four short BF16 output rows per block when `in_f <= 2048`; `MMV_BLOCK` selects the BF16 matvec block size (64..256, multiple of 32). Both change kernel scheduling; require a fresh tape and battery before promotion. |
| `MEMRA_EXACT16_MOE` | off | Admit the exact-width-16 MoE decode tier. It is byte-safe but currently slower than two width-8 waves, so it remains an explicit measurement door. |
| `MEMRA_FA_DCW_MEMSET` | on | `0` skips three dead partial-pool memsets in DCW attention. Token-graph capture forces them back on because graph retargeting identifies the attention children by that signature. |
| `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT` / `MEMRA_OPROJ_TAIL` / `MEMRA_SHEXP_SPLIT` | off | Step TP probes for splitting the LM head, deferring the output-projection join into the residual/norm consumer, and splitting the shared-expert down rows. Each falls back when its exact geometry is ineligible. |
| `MEMRA_LOAD_MTP` | off | `run-gen` directory-checkpoint input: `1` loads embedded NextN/MTP heads instead of the trunk-only loader so `MEMRA_SPEC_K` can exercise the attached chain. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_GRAPH_LOOP` / `MEMRA_STEP_TP_GRAPH_DEBUG` | 0 / off | Opt-in Step TP token-graph chunk length (`>=2` enables) and per-token rank counter dump. The debug arm performs device-to-host reads and is never a measured configuration. |
| `MEMRA_MOE_CSR_NVFP4` | off | Diagnostic re-admission of NVFP4 experts to the CSR dedup arm through exact-width-16 decode. The cached form remains rejected by batch-composition identity gates; never enable in serving. |
| `MEMRA_NO_LOCAL_SHADOW` | off | Skip Step TP eager local-KV shadow gathers/appends while advancing their lengths. The local contents feed spec/MTP scratch, so this is a decode-only diagnostic door. |
| `MEMRA_SEL_MR` / `MEMRA_SEL_STREAM` / `MEMRA_SEL_V2S` | off | NVFP4 selected-row kernel probes: four concurrent row groups, 16-row streaming, or the v2-bank eight-row streaming twin. The MR form measured slower; the streaming forms are geometry-limited and remain opt-in. |
| `MEMRA_SIG_EXPF_DEV` / `MEMRA_TOPK_FAST` | off | Router numeric-class and scheduling probes: device-libm sigmoid `expf`, and the barrier-lean warp-local top-k merge. Both require a new tape plus the full battery. |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER` / `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL` / `MEMRA_TCOL_REFKERN` | off | Step serving-class verification: row-outer replay of the live t=1 program; optional T-column QKV amortization at T=2..8; and the per-column t=1 reference-kernel bisect for T-column slabs. `TCOL` is subordinate to `EAGER`; `REFKERN` is diagnosis only. |
| `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ` / `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ_REF` / `MEMRA_TCOL_PROF` | off | Tcol verify-walk o_proj levers: `OPROJ` defers each column's o_proj into one batched b4_tcol + slab join per layer (measured -6.5% at K=1 — door stays OFF until the FFN side is also column-batched); `OPROJ_REF` fills the partial slab via the t=1 b4 kernel per column, the permanent choreography-vs-kernel bisect twin; `PROF` prints a synchronized per-segment wall split of the walk — the syncs serialize the stream, so it targets amortization work and is never a perf claim. |
| `MEMRA_TCOL_FFN` | off | Tcol verify-walk MoE FFN lever (`1` implies the `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ` defer): when every column of a MoE layer deferred its o_proj, the residual norm runs as one t-grid launch (per-row program == t=1) and the FFN as ONE two-column device-routed sweep + per-column shexp — the columns' routed-expert weight reads dedup through L2 instead of paying HBM twice. Exactness banked 2026-08-23: 200-token greedy tape byte-match first run. Door stays OFF and unbenchmarked (single run 43.74 vs 47.9 tok/s on drifting clocks is not a measurement); banked for when the spec lane reopens. |
| `MEMRA_TAIL_ADD3` | on | `0` disables the MoE tail fusion that folds the shared-expert apply into the routed join add. Default-on identity-preserving launch reduction; zero is its rollback seam. |
| `MEMRA_TG_PROBE_LAYER` | none | Token-graph bisection layer index. Dumps the eager attention output and post-FFN residual at that layer, adding synchronization and host I/O. |
