| `MEMRA_MOE_GHOST` + `MEMRA_MOE_FAST_ADMIT` | second-miss ghost filter: net loss in BOTH regimes (spill +3.4% with it off; 96GB bypassed it) — first-miss admit is the only policy, FAST_ADMIT became a no-op | rig5090 2026-07-06 / rtx6000 2026-07-04 |
| `MEMRA_MOE_ORDER` | descending-m_e expert order won (1.34x first-forward); the `=id` restore arm was dead | rig5090 2026-07-04 |
| `MEMRA_FA_VEC` | vestigial: dispatch reads `MEMRA_NO_FA_VEC` since the 2026-06-28 default-flip. Writes removed; the kernel-check scalar-vs-vec gate now toggles `MEMRA_NO_FA_VEC` (the old toggle had gone vacuous) | audit 2026-07-08 |
| `MEMRA_GEMM` | zero read sites (gate went unconditional when MMQ Phase 0 shipped); writes removed from scripts/docs | audit 2026-07-08 |
| `MEMRA_NO_EVT` | legacy no-op alias of the EVT elision default | audit 2026-07-08 |
| `MEMRA_MOE_GDEC_GATE` | documented as a live byte-identity oracle (grouped-decode vs sequential-axpy) but had **zero read sites** — the only trace in the tree is the comment at `hybrid_forward.rs:2510` describing what it would compare. The identity itself is still asserted, by construction rather than by a gate: the grouped down-accumulation is a slot-ordered `__fmaf_rn` chain equal to the sequential `axpy_f32` chain, each in-kernel dot reproduces `qmatvec_f32`'s reduction, and `MEMRA_MOE_GDEC=0` (§2) remains the live rollback arm. Delisted, not replaced | audit 2026-08-07 (docs-fit) |

Bench bins deleted with their flags: `q4v_bench`, `q6iss_bench`, `fa_ppool_bench` (superseded by
`fa_v2_bench`, which keeps the FA depth/split/smem sweep).

## Step TP doors (LIVE flags — the step37 2-card serving stack; not part of the removed ledger above)

These rows were appended after the removed-flags ledger and are live doors, not concluded
ones — filed under their own heading so a flag audit scanning the live sections sees them.

| flag | default | what it does |
|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_STEP_NVFP4_DEV_ROUTES` | off | Strict `0`/`1` device-resident NVFP4 TP2 routed-expert decode program (t=1 rows; prefill and t>1 keep the host-canonical program). The gate/up column halves land on the rank owning the matching down canonical shard, so the expert interior is rank-local with zero cross-rank transfer; one q8_1 quantization per rank is shared across every selected expert, `silu_mul_scaled_q8_1` folds the gate/up macros and emits down's operand directly, and the down macro folds into the route-weight axpy scalar (exact). Numeric class = device silu + folded macros (owning-stage dev-path semantics), NOT bit-equal to the host-canonical oracle; gated by prefill/decode argmax MATCH and host-oracle A/B (identical argmax, same logit-maxdiff class). Measured on the 2x RTX PRO 6000 pair, full TP 0-42 + EP tail: host-canonical 7.8 tok/s -> 11.1 tok/s. `MEMRA_STEP_TP_TIMING=1` prints cumulative program timings (this door + the rank-local attention decode) for wall decomposition: attention 1550us/layer = 78% of the 90ms decode wall, experts 13% — the next perf rung is attention residency + one-shot P2P (design: `research/step37-nvfp4-tp2-20260820/ENGINE-BASICS-SWEEP.md`), not more expert-side work. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_TIMING` | off | `1` prints cumulative wall-clock counters for the NVFP4 device routes program and the rank-local TP attention decode, plus an 8-phase split of the v1 attention driver (pos/qkv/normrope/gate/append/attn/oproj/shadow; each phase lap drains every stream, so the split perturbs absolute wall but attributes queued work honestly). Diagnostic only; never on in a gated or measured-for-receipts run. |
| `MEMRA_BF16_MMV` | off | Strict `0`/`1`. At LOAD, large (>=2M elements) 2D BF16 checkpoint tensors stay bf16-resident (`GpuTensor::FloatBf16`, raw bytes, 2 B/w — halves both VRAM and decode read traffic for every preserved non-expert weight: shared expert, lm_head, owning-stage attention, dense FFN); the MoE router (1.18M) deliberately stays exact-f32 so routing never moves. At DECODE (m=1, non-exact, non-canonical) those projections ride a one-block-per-row bf16 matvec (`matvec_bf16_f32acc`, f32 accumulate, deterministic tree reduce, same bits<<16 expansion contract as `deq`); m>1 (prefill/verify) rides the chunked expansion path. Numeric-class door vs the f32-expanded cuBLASLt program — default OFF, run-gen argmax gate + 12-boot battery (DEV_ROUTES acceptance class). The step TP canonical-chunk ranks are explicitly excluded. Measured with the full v2 stack: 25.45 -> 27.22 tok/s, head 1.40 -> 0.74 ms/token. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DEV_ROUTER` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: routes the step TP MoE selection on device (`moe_router_sigmoid_topk`, the single-GPU dev arm's router) and feeds the device-routed expert program — the per-layer host logits readback (the last per-layer host sync in the MoE path) disappears, which is the prerequisite for whole-token CUDA-graph capture. Selection tie-breaking may differ from the host router: numeric-class door, run-gen argmax gate + 12-boot battery. Requires `MEMRA_STEP_NVFP4_DEV_ROUTES=1`. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: inside the v2 decode driver, ONE launch per rank computes all three rank-local QKV projections (`matvec_f32_qkv`, block-per-row over the F32 mirror, f32 accumulate, deterministic tree reduce) instead of ~12 chunked cuBLASLt calls per rank. NUMERIC-CLASS door (per-row reduction order differs from cuBLASLt): measured greedy tie-flip at depth 58/64, gate argmax MATCH, maxdiff class 2.4e-3 -> 4.2e-3. Requires `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DECODE_V2=1`. Acceptance = run-gen argmax gate + 12-boot battery (DEV_ROUTES class). 22.88 -> 24.99 tok/s on the 2x6000 pair. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DECODE_V2` | off | Strict `0`/`1` v2 rank-local Step decode-attention driver: the same kernels, operand values, and canonical O reduction order as the v1 driver, restructured onto a persistent per-runtime workspace with evented cross-stream ordering (pp.rs `BoundarySlot` discipline). Per layer per token it performs exactly one cuMemAlloc (the returned output row), no host round-trip, and no host stream sync — v1 spends 81% of its 1550us/layer wall on those classes (see the phase timers). Requires `MEMRA_STEP_TP_F32_MIRROR=1` (refused otherwise: the bit-exactness claim against v1 holds per residency class) and the replicated decode input. Prefill and every non-attention program are unchanged. Gate: v1-vs-v2 greedy token identity + the run-gen prefill/decode argmax gate across a boot battery. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DCW` | off | Strict `0`/`1` device-counter-windowed step TP decode attention: KV append at `row = len_d - base_d` in-kernel, `inc_i32` advances the device counter in-stream, and `fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw` derives the attention view from the counters (one-partition law: at matching `(bucket, split_keys)` bit-identical to the host-planned path). Rebase tokens and sub-vec-floor contexts fall back to the host-row path. Requires the v2 driver + fused doors. Gate: 200-token identity vs the dcw-off tape + 12-boot battery. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_GRAPH` | off | Strict `0`/`1` whole-token stitched CUDA graph for step TP decode (increment B/C): every e/rank/root section of a token captures as an alloc-free child (no warmups — bodies carry dcw appends), assembled as one multi-device parent, launched once per token; bucket changes retarget the SAME exec via kernel/memset param updates (M1 path) instead of rebuilds. Eligibility: dcw + fused + dev-router doors, depth >= 96, no pending rebase; eager fallback otherwise. Host bookkeeping overlaps the replay; commit skips len-mirror sets (`commit_tp_kv_transaction_external` — the in-graph incs own the counters) with a rank-stream fence at eager->graph seams. Gate: 12-boot 200-token identity battery (12/12, 2026-08-21) + 400-token identity through 22 bucket retargets. Measured 40.6 tok/s vs eager 42.0 at depth 424 (interleaved x3): the launch itself costs ~1.3 ms/token at ~405 children — the door earns its keep with the multi-token replay loop, not plain decode. **RE-MEASURED ON THE CURRENT STACK 2026-08-25** (that verdict was taken when a token cost ~24 ms; today it costs 13.2 ms with ~6 ms of non-streaming remainder, so the tax was being weighed against a different total): ctx 4081, interleaved x3, token ids identical in every arm — eager 75.99 / 75.98 / 75.84 vs graph 59.39 / 61.27 / 61.09 tok/s, **-20%**. The remainder is dependency LATENCY (405 small serialized kernels that cannot fill the device), not launch overhead, so graph replay cannot recover it. Default OFF. |
| `MEMRA_RMS_BLOCK` | 256 | RMS-norm block size (existing tuning env). step37 2x RTX PRO 6000 serving finding (2026-08-21): `1024` cuts the latency-bound t=1 norms (19us -> single-block 4096-elem class) and lifts the full step37 serving stack 42.0 -> 44.0 tok/s eager / 40.6 -> 42.1 graph (interleaved x2, reproducible). NUMERIC-CLASS change (reduction stride): the serving env pins it explicitly; acceptance = fresh-tape identity gate + 12-boot battery at the pinned value. |
| `MEMRA_NVFP4_BANK_V2` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: stores the step TP contiguous NVFP4 expert banks (gate/up/down) in a SLOT-MAJOR row layout — slot g's 16 qs bytes contiguous at `g*16` (one coalesced 512B warp wave), the two UE4M3 scale bytes packed at `nslots*16 + g*2` — and dispatches the `*_v2` kernels (`qmatvec_nvfp4_dp4a_v2`, `_sel_v2`, `qmatvec_nvfp4_fast_v2` for the host-canonical bank oracles). Pure storage permutation: per-slot dp4a and scale order unchanged, outputs BIT-IDENTICAL to v1 (gated against the banked tape). Non-bank NVFP4 buffers stay v1. Measured +1.0 tok/s on the step37 2x RTX PRO 6000 serving stack (46.5 -> 47.6 eager, 44.7 -> 45.7 graph, interleaved x2); 12-boot battery 12/12 at the full serving env. Default OFF; the step37 serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_OPROJ_DIRECT` | off | Strict `0`/`1` o-proj DIRECT JOIN (step TP2 fused arm): peer ranks write their fused b4 O partial OVER P2P into a root-resident buffer (UVA kernel stores; ws allocates peer partials on root), and the model engine adds the two partials straight into its own output row — the root stream leaves the join (no peer pull copy, no root add, one event hop, no 16KB ownership copy). Same operands, same add order: BIT-IDENTICAL. +0.6 on the step37 2x RTX PRO 6000 stack (57.1 -> 57.7, interleaved x3); battery v14 12/12 incl. graph capture. Default OFF; the step37 serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_MOE_DIRECT` | off | Strict `0`/`1` moe DIRECT JOIN: the o-proj recipe on the expert combine — peer route accumulators live root-side (`axpy_rows_seq_md` register-accumulates and stores ONCE, so the P2P cost is a single 16KB pass) and e adds the shard rows itself into a FRESH e-context row. WAR safety via the ev_entry transitivity chain. BIT-IDENTICAL (+1.85, 57.9 -> 59.8); battery v15 12/12. Default OFF; serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_ROUTES_PRESTAGE` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: stage the routed-expert input to every rank and quantize it BEFORE the router runs — neither depends on the selection, so the rank chains overlap the router gemv+topk instead of chaining behind the entry event. Same copies, same quantize kernel: BIT-IDENTICAL (+2.5, 59.6 -> 62.0); battery v16 12/12. Default OFF; serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_SHEXP_OVERLAP` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: issue the shared expert (dual matvec+SwiGLU, split down) on e's stream from the routes PREJOIN hook — it executes while the peer rank drains its sweep, filling dev0's join wait; `add_scaled_rows` applies the row after the join. Split program receipted bit-identical to fused #2e. The hook placement is LOAD-BEARING: after rank issue (early placement starved dev1, -8). +5.1 (62.2 -> 67.3), the largest single lever of the campaign; battery v17 12/12. Default OFF; serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_ASYNC_CHAIN` | 0 | `K>=2` enables `run-gen`'s eager device-chained chunk (`decode_step_chain`; memra-server has no caller and does not arm this flag). Greedy writes tail argmax into resident `token_d`; sampled decode performs `filter_stats` -> `gumbel_perturb_filtered_col` -> argmax inside the same chain, with a per-step counter, so neither mode host-syncs between tokens. When `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT=1`, sampled decode draws from the split path's materialized concatenated row through `head_split_sample_device`; it no longer drops to the plain head. The older +0.6 tok/s at K=8 (K=16 flat; battery v18 12/12) is a greedy `run-gen` receipt, not a serving claim. Default 0. |
| `MEMRA_LEN_MIRROR_LAZY` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: skip the ~3x45 per-token 4-byte IN-STREAM len htods — the LOCAL device mirror is unread in TP decode, and under `MEMRA_FUSE_ROPE_APPEND` the fused append's atomicInc owns the rank counters (the commit re-set is the same value on every fully-accepted greedy token; rollback paths keep absolute sets). CAVEAT: must drop — or spec must own its mirrors — when MTP partial-accept lands. +1.1 (67.9 -> 69.0); battery v20 12/12. Default OFF; serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_DEV1_ROUTER` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: rank1 routes LOCALLY from its own z copy via per-layer router replicas (gate_inp f32 + exp_probs_b + active mask, one-time P2P upload) — deterministic kernels on identical input bits produce bit-equal sel/w, so rank1's sweep starts without waiting the root's router+broadcast. Rides the prestage hook. +0.6 (69.1 -> 69.7); battery v21. Default OFF; serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_FUSE_ADD_NORM` | on | `0` reverts FUSION #2f: off the q8_1 fast path the cross-layer residual add + attn_norm ride one `add_rms_norm` launch (kernel_check identity, same rms_block). BIT-IDENTICAL; +0.4. |
| `MEMRA_FUSE_DOWN_ADDSCALE` | on | `0` reverts FUSION #2e: gate-less shexp down matvec + scaled accumulate straight into moe_out in ONE launch (exact f32acc per-row program + exact add_scaled expression). BIT-IDENTICAL; +0.6. (The shexp-overlap door uses the split program instead — identical values either way.) |
| `MEMRA_B4_X2` | off | RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (-0.2): b4 grid-halving two-rows-per-block twin. The x2 shape pays only at SMALL out_f (shexp 1280: +0.95 as part of `matvec_bf16_dual_silu`); at out_f=4096 the occupancy loss beats the reduce-tail hiding. Do not re-flip on big-out matvecs without a new receipt. |
| `MEMRA_DEV_EMBED` | off | RECEIPTED FLAT: device embed gather in eager decode (bit-identical rows) — the host row expand fully overlaps GPU work and the resident table costs ~2.1GB. The CHAIN door uses device gather regardless (it must). |
| `MEMRA_FENCE_MEMOPS` | off | RECEIPTED FLAT on PCIe: doorbell (cuStreamWriteValue32) for the root->e join hop. Stream memops are REJECTED on peer memory over PCIe P2P and on async-pool allocations (both CUDA_ERROR_INVALID_VALUE; the flag rides raw cuMemAlloc). Kept for NVLink-class fabrics. |
| `MEMRA_RANK0_MERGE` | off | RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (-2.7): same-device rank0 riding e's stream via the runtime redirect (legal — same primary context). dev0's two-stream concurrency is real; prestage rides exactly that seam. |
| `MEMRA_SHEXP_DEV1` | off | RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (~-5, structural): the shared expert on rank1 extends the join critical path — dev1's idle window sits BEFORE z exists, so dev0's peer-drain slot (used by `MEMRA_SHEXP_OVERLAP`) is the only shexp slot. Per-layer replica machinery kept. |
| `MEMRA_SEL_DOWN8` | off | Strict `1`: NVFP4 routed DOWN sweep + route-weight combine in ONE launch, block `(32, n_sel)` — one warp per SLOT instead of one warp per `(row, slot)` (the q8 `moe_down8` w8 occupancy arm ported to the NVFP4 bank family, which had kept the base shape plus a separate `axpy_rows_seq_md` over an `n_sel x out_f` partial buffer). Per-slot FP chain, 32-lane reduce and slot-ordered accumulation are the two-launch pair's verbatim, so outputs are BIT-IDENTICAL (200-tok tape EXACT). Device-routed arm only (the host arm folds the down macro into `combine_w`); needs `nsb <= 32` and the v2 banks. Measured +5.5% at turn8 agentic context (75.12/74.80/74.81 vs 70.86/71.08/70.94, interleaved x3, no overlap) — nsys had the base down sweep at 321 GB/s, 18% of this pair's GDDR7 peak, against 792 GB/s for the gate/up twin: latency/occupancy, not bandwidth. Default OFF pending the 12-boot battery; the step37 serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_FA_COMBINE_S` | off | Strict `1`: split-combine twin that stages `partM`/`partL` into dynamic shared memory once, cooperatively, instead of having EVERY thread walk them from global twice (the first pass is an `n_splits`-deep DEPENDENT load chain per thread — 11.4us per layer for a few hundred flops). Same `s`-ascending order for the max, the `l` sum and the `o` sum: BIT-IDENTICAL (tape EXACT). +0.9% at turn8 (75.84/76.12 vs 75.26/75.30, no overlap). Default OFF pending the battery; the step37 serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_SEL_MIRROR` | off | Strict `1`: the per-rank routed-selection pull runs as ONE `moe_sel_w_mirror` launch instead of two 32-BYTE D2D copies, and when every consuming rank shares e's device the e-context staging pair is skipped (the caller's selection rows are process-persistent, so ranks read them directly). nsys counted 167 such copies/token at 3.79us each for 32 bytes of payload. Bit-identical; refused under the graph door, whose captured copies need the fixed staging addresses. +0.67% at turn8 (75.25/75.32/75.63 vs 74.70/74.85/75.15) — most of that copy time overlaps compute. Default OFF; the step37 serving env pins it on. |
| `MEMRA_FA_UNROLL` | 4 | `8` selects the B1-unroll-8 decode-attention twin (`fa_dec_v3_walk_u<8>`). fa loads K inside a sequential per-key loop and nsys shows ~1.3us PER KEY (23us at a 16-key split, 84.2us at 68) — latency, not bandwidth — so a deeper load pipeline looked free. RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (-2.2%: 73.08/73.30/73.68 vs 74.36/75.04/75.14); bit-identical (tape EXACT). The walk is templated on the factor so both coexist; the default path is unchanged. |
| `MEMRA_FA_SP_SHORT` | unset | Split-keys override for the SHORT rung only (`t_kv <= 2048`, which is where step37's 33 SWA layers land at their capped 512) — the one thing a global `MEMRA_FA_SPLIT` cannot isolate, since it moves the deep-ctx rung too. Tests the full-tile argument (`FA_DEC_TILE` is 32, so split 16 runs half-empty tiles). RECEIPTED NEGATIVE: `32` -> 72.20/72.38, `8` -> 74.51/74.51 vs the `16` default -> 74.70/74.77. With this the ladder is measured at 8/16/32/48/64/96 and closed. NUMERIC-CLASS (key partition) — a default change would need a fresh tape plus the battery. |
| `MEMRA_SEL_GU_RPW` | 1 | `2`/`4` select the gate/up multirow twins (the q8 `gu_geom<RPW>` arm): one block covers RPW consecutive output rows of one slot so each thread's activation group is read once and reused across the RPW gate+up dots. The base kernel re-reads 47 MB/layer/rank of activation from L2 against 23.6 MB of weights, so this should have paid. RECEIPTED: `2` FLAT (74.77/74.68), `4` -3%. Both bit-identical (tape EXACT) — the gu sweep is not L2-activation-bound. |
| `MEMRA_FENCE_RANK1` | off | Strict `1`: the peer rank rings a doorbell in ROOT memory with a fenced kernel store (`memra_ring_flag`) and the model engine waits it with a SAME-DEVICE `cuStreamWaitValue32(GEQ)` instead of a cross-device event — the half `MEMRA_FENCE_MEMOPS`' receipt left unbuilt (peer memops are rejected; peer kernel stores are the direct-join mechanism). Requires `MEMRA_FENCE_MEMOPS=1`, which owns the flag allocation. Ordering only, byte-exact (tape EXACT), and RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (75.13/75.25 vs base 75.89/75.83/75.95; the root-only half re-receipts at 75.56/75.56): the ring launch plus the memop poll cost more than the event they replace, so event signalling on this PCIe pair is already at the floor. Kept as the measured seam for an NVLink-class fabric. |
| `MEMRA_FA_HOIST` | off | `1` hoists B1's K-read alignment CLASS out of the per-key loop (it is invariant — `k_tok_bytes % 4 == 0`, so every key shares the lane's residue — but it is computed from a per-key pointer, so the compiler cannot prove it and versions nothing); `2` additionally replaces the `memcpy(&w, ap, 4)` word reads with typed 4-byte loads (memcpy from a `uint8_t*` may lower to four byte loads; the rest of this file uses `__ldcs((const int*)p)`). Both bit-identical (tape EXACT). RECEIPTED: `1` -2.3% (74.22/74.33/74.50 vs 75.98/75.92/76.07), `2` FLAT (75.89 vs 75.86) — the typed load recovers exactly what the hoisted version loses. Together with `MEMRA_FA_UNROLL=8` (-2.2%) this closes the load-issue hypothesis for the decode-attention walk: three independent attempts to deepen or clean its K load pipeline move nothing, so its ~1.18us/key is not load-issue-bound. ncu is permission-blocked in this container (`ERR_NVGPUCTRPERM`), so the next step needs counters or a structural rewrite. |
| `MEMRA_FA_PROF` | off | `1` runs the clock64()-instrumented decode-attention walk (`fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw_prof`): each block's first-warp leader stamps the walk's phase boundaries and atomically accumulates cycle deltas into an 8-slot buffer, printed as shares every 430 launches. Exists because **ncu cannot run in this container** (`ERR_NVGPUCTRPERM`, and `/sys/module/nvidia/parameters` is not exposed so the restriction cannot be lifted from inside) — clock64 needs no counter permission. Diagnostic only (extra atomics per block; never a measured configuration). Measured on step37 at turn8 context: `b1_klo=59-63%` (K load + dp4a dots), `stageV=19%`, `b3_vacc=16-18%`, `b2_soft=1.5-3%`, `sync=0.4-0.6%`, ~790-810 cycles/key. |
| `MEMRA_FA_HSPLIT` | off | `2` splits each kv_head's gqa warp group across TWO blocks (grid `2*n_head_kv x n_splits`, each block staging its own sV copy of the same keys) — bit-identical per `(head, split)`, only the hosting block changes. Tests whether B1's 59-63% cycle share is occupancy-starved latency: this rank has only `n_head_kv=4`, so the base grid is 4 x n_splits at ~16% occupancy. RECEIPTED NEGATIVE (-2.7%: 73.86/73.84 vs 75.79/75.96/76.04; identity EXACT) — the duplicated Phase A dequant costs more than the extra parallelism buys. With the split ladder (closed at 8..96), `MEMRA_FA_UNROLL` and `MEMRA_FA_HOIST`, this is the fourth falsified hypothesis for the walk's per-key cost; what remains changes the dot's data layout (key-per-lane), which moves its FP order. |
| `MEMRA_MAX_CTX` | unset | Provision the KV/ring caches for N tokens instead of just this run's `prompt + generation` (the value is clamped UP to the run's own requirement, so it can never under-allocate). `run-gen` sized `max_ctx` to the fixture, which is NOT the serving configuration — a server provisions the model's supported window up front and decode pays for that footprint (allocation, ring geometry, TLB/L2). Any perf number intended to describe serving must state the provisioned context; step37-flash supports 262144. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8` | off | Strict `1`: q8_0 mirror of the step TP attention projections for DECODE. At load each resident bf16 rank shard gets a q8_0 twin (`encode_q8_0_rows_from_bf16`, the same 34-byte `[half d][32 x i8]` block program `quant_K_block` writes for the KV cache); the decode QKV step then quantizes its activation with the existing `quantize_q8_1` and issues one `mmvq` per Q/K/V instead of the fused bf16 `qkvg` — the gate rows (32 wide) stay bf16, since there is nothing to win there and one fewer class to qualify. The bf16 slabs stay resident, so every prefill and verify path keeps the arithmetic it was qualified against. **NUMERIC-CLASS door**, same class and acceptance as `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED` / `MEMRA_BF16_MMV` / `MEMRA_SEL_GU_WPR` (per-row arithmetic becomes an int8 dp4a dot with per-32 scales, so a bit-tape cannot apply): acceptance is the run-gen argmax gate with its logit maxdiff plus the boot battery, and the default stays OFF until the owner ratifies. Measured motive (`decode-kernel-census` 2026-08-25): the fused qkv shape is 23.0 us bf16 at 1.83 TB/s vs 14.0 us q8_0 at 1.60, and o_proj 24.2 -> 11.7 us; the two together are ~-1.0 ms of a 13.16 ms token, the largest lever left after every bit-exact knob measured flat (TOPK_FAST +0.2%, DOWN_X4 +0.5%, SEL_GU_RPW flat, graph door -20%). **GATED AND MEASURED 2026-08-25** (box tip 12adfd46b): run-gen argmax gate MATCH in every cell (`verify-prefill argmax=70935 decode argmax=70935`) at logit maxdiff **9.798e-1** at ctx 193 and **1.570e0** at ctx 4081 — note this is ~200x the 4.2e-3 that QKV_FUSED and SEL_GU_WPR carry, because this changes weight PRECISION, not just reduction order, so ratification needs a quality battery and not just the argmax line. SPEED: ctx 193 80.97 -> 83.18 tok/s (+2.7%); ctx 4081 interleaved x3 75.76/75.91/75.73 -> 78.01/78.01/77.86 (**+2.9%**). The first wiring issued one mmvq per projection and measured 79.52 vs 80.72 — SLOWER — because three launches plus the activation quantize cost more than the halved bytes save; `qmatvec_q8_0_qkv_rp` (one launch, bit-identical to those three) is what makes the door positive. RESIDENCY CONSTRAINT: the mirror is ~0.9 GB per card and does NOT fit beside a 262144-token cache reservation on 96 GB — the first long-context cell died in CUDA_ERROR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, and the interleave above therefore ran MEMRA_MAX_CTX=16384 on BOTH arms. A serving flip needs either a smaller ctx reservation or dropping the bf16 slab for decode-only ranks. The door also covers the bf16 GEMVs OUTSIDE the TP banks — the LM head, the shared-expert rows and the dense-FFN layers — through a pointer-keyed mirror cache built on first decode use (`matvec_bf16_via_q8_mirror`), which adds roughly 1.7 GB per card on top of the 0.9 GB the attention mirrors cost. **BOTH ARMS (QKV + o_proj), ctx 4081, interleaved x3, tip 98d601256:** off 75.74/75.78/75.93 -> on 80.27/80.78/80.29 tok/s = **+6.1%**, argmax gate MATCH in every cell at logit maxdiff 2.055e0. That is 12.46 ms/token against the 13.19 ms baseline; 90 tok/s needs 11.1 ms, so the measured remainder is a q8 head (-0.16 ms), q8 shexp down (-0.33), q8 dense FFN on layers 0-2 (~-0.22) and then a W4-class attention projection running at the ~1.9 TB/s the q8 mmvq path already demonstrates (~-0.48). |
| `MEMRA_SEL_GU_WPR` | off | Strict `1`: warp-per-row NVFP4 gate/up sweep — one WARP owns an output row (4 slots per lane at in_f=4096, warp-only reduce, no shared memory and no barrier), `blockDim.y` packing 4 rows per block. NUMERIC-CLASS door, same class and acceptance as `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED` / `MEMRA_BF16_MMV` / `MEMRA_RMS_BLOCK` (the per-row REDUCTION ORDER changes; a bit-tape cannot apply): run-gen prefill/decode argmax **MATCH** at `logit maxdiff=4.494e-3`, the same class QKV_FUSED documents at 4.2e-3, plus the boot battery before any default change. Rationale: the base kernel gives one row to 128 threads, so at nsb=128 each thread owns ONE slot — one vectorized load — and then pays a two-stage reduce (5 shfl + shared + `__syncthreads` + 5 shfl); nsys had it at 792 GB/s, 44% of peak, while the wide-row members of the same family sit at roofline (lm_head 1.62 TB/s, dense FFN 1.51). MEASURED +0.4% only (76.09/76.15/76.20 vs 75.86/75.70/76.03, monotone): the reduce was NOT the dominant cost, so with `MEMRA_SEL_GU_RPW` flat this kernel is at its dp4a/LUT-decode ceiling, not a shape or reduce ceiling. Default OFF — 0.3 tok/s does not justify a numeric class. |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2` | off | T-ROW verify attention over the per-session fa table kernel (supersedes the dcw2 pair-join 2026-08-24): the walk stashes q+gate per column (append lands per column), then ONE `fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw_rows` per rank attends every row — each (row, head, split) block IS the t=1 dcw program with that row's own ring/len/base and split geometry (len_back = t-1-r for same-session verify rows), so per-row bit-identity holds at ANY t in [2,8] INCLUDING capped SWA (the dcw2 partition guard + SWA refusal dissolved; same-session rows dedup K/V through L2). Combine = t=1 kernel over t*n_head stacked heads (empty splits are the NEG_INF no-op). Launcher embeds the big-rig split ladder and refuses env split overrides. GATES: run-spec SELF-CONSISTENCY PASS K=1..8 (tiny) + K=1,2 at agentic ctx. Spec ladder at agentic turn-8 ctx: 50.6 -> 54.4 (rows fa) -> 62.4 (head convert killed: the FloatBf16 chunked matmul dequanted the whole 1.24GB LM head per verify round; bf16 rows fast path = t=1 head program per row) -> 66.87 (verify rope pass: same-session rope-rows, 3 launches/rank/layer) = 0.97x eager at accept 89.6%. Requires MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER=1 + MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL=1. |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2_DEBUG` | off | Prints the FIRST failing precheck clause once per clause id (`[spec-fa2] precheck FAIL clause=...`) — a silently-vacuous fa2 door is indistinguishable from a slow one without it. Diagnostic only; no behavior change. |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2_LAYER` | unset | Debug bisection: engage the fa2 join on ONE layer index only (all other layers run the ordinary per-column program). Divergence isolation; no use in serving. |
| `MEMRA_TN_TRACE` | off | Stage probes in the t-row verify MoE (`[tn-trace]` at logits/topk/driver/shexp). Diagnostic only. |
| `MEMRA_SWEEP_TRACE` | off | One-time `[sweep-trace]` receipt of the eager down8 dispatch inputs (device_routed / door / local_in). Diagnostic only. |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS` | off | **SERVING RECEIPTS 2026-08-25 (tip affb4a081, 2-card box, streaming, C=1, temp 0, two interleaved rounds):** TTFT 11.16->2.375 s at 182 prompt tokens, 73.51->10.27 s at 1271, and 4081 tokens went from HTTP 408 (prime could not make first token inside the 90 s `timeout_ms` ceiling — UNSERVABLE) to 32.30 s served. Prime rate 17.2->126 tok/s. IDENTITY: the served token ids match the tokenwise-class run-gen reference exactly `[764,624,15059,2859,223,19,63,342]`; the server's own host-canonical batched prime is the outlier (`20` at slot 6), so this arm moves serving TOWARD the oracle. Still off by default pending the owner's flip. PREFILL-BY-T-ROWS (the #2 serving arc, 2026-08-25): prime the prompt through the same-session t-row walk in 32-row chunks — one rope/append + one fa + one o_proj join + one t-row MoE sweep per rank per layer per chunk, every row the t=1 decode program bit-for-bit. This is the TOKENWISE-prime ORACLE class (strictly more conservative than the batched host-canonical prime it replaces), so the gate is token identity vs MEMRA_PRIME_TOKENWISE=1. Motivation receipt: step37 TP2 host-canonical prime = ~17 tok/s (4.1k prompt = ~240 s TTFT; the memra-server bench 408d on it) vs vLLM prefill 0.6 s. Requires MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER=1 + MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL=1 (+ TCOL_FFN/SPEC_FA2 for speed); text-only fresh primes; suffix/vision/dflash-tap primes fall through. |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS_T` | 8 | Chunk width for `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS`. Unset defaults to 8; configured values must be an integer in `2..=8` or the request is refused by name. `matvec_bf16_qkvg_tcol_into` cannot execute `t>8`, so the historical width-32 trial failed before it became a supported recipe. Measured at 193 prompt tokens: width 8 = 2.459 s, width 4 = 2.574 s. Diagnostic knob; shipped width is 8. |
| `MEMRA_TEMP` / `MEMRA_TOP_K` / `MEMRA_TOP_P` / `MEMRA_MIN_P` / `MEMRA_SEED` (safetensors path) | greedy | **FIXED 2026-08-25:** the safetensors TEXT path IGNORED these and still printed `(ST greedy decode)`, so every sampled step37 measurement taken with run-gen was really greedy — the sampler it parsed belonged to the GGUF `generate_with` path. `MEMRA_TEMP>0` now arms the serving-equivalent device sampler INSIDE the async chain (`filter_stats` -> `gumbel_perturb_filtered_col` -> argmax into the resident `token_d`), so a sampled stream keeps the chain's no-host-sync property and the per-step counter gives each token its own draw. With `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT=1`, sampling uses the full concatenated split-head row and stays on the split path. The bench line now names the shape it measured. Owner rule this serves: "we dont serve greedy, for real benchmarking we use sampling". |
| `MEMRA_W8_TRACE` | off | One `[w8-mirror] built in_f=.. out_f=.. mirrors=N` line per q8_0 mirror the W8 hybrid half builds. Diagnostic: the head and shared expert may reach the device through the rows fast path or the fused dual-silu launcher rather than `matvec_bf16_into`, and the hybrid half measured +0.1%, so this is how you tell whether it fired at all. |
| `MEMRA_RAW_COPY_TRACE` | off | Print operands + backtrace when `tp::raw_copy_bytes` fails (a raw D2D carries no call site otherwise). Diagnostic only; the returned error string now always carries bytes/dst/src. |
| `MEMRA_ROPE_ROWS` | on under MEMRA_STEP_TP_BATCH (=0 reverts to the stash flow) | Batched-walk FULL t-row attention pass: rope/append + fa + combine + o_proj join in 3 launches/rank/layer over the shared 6-word session table (per-row planes/len/base/done_ctr; positions in a per-tick slab staged on the FIRST ENGAGED layer — staging only at il==lo was the diverged-fingerprint bug: a first-layer refusal left every engaged layer roping at pos=0). GATED 2026-08-24: TOKFP identity vs the replay baseline at B=2/4 (ctx512) and B=8 (ctx4400); aggregate 72.1->97.3 (B=4 ctx512) and 95.9->109.2 (B=8@4400, 73.2 ms/tick). |
| `MEMRA_ROPE_ROWS_LAYER` | unset | Debug bisection: engage the full t-row pass on ONE layer only. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_BATCH` | off | **Current correctness posture (Hermes `64fa2b55baf0d887`): compile-time-T 2/4/8 twins are research-only and are not dispatched; every live width uses the runtime-T kernel so changing batch width cannot switch a request's numeric program. Performance rows that include the twins are historical and require fresh TOKFP plus serving requalification before reuse.** T-ROW BATCHED STEP-TP WALK (owner directive 2026-08-24, the missing primitive for serving AND verify): B<=8 rows advance one LAYER at a time through per-row-exact twins — one t-grid attn norm + one weight-amortized QKV tcol over all rows, per-row attention on its OWN session cache (unmodified t=1 program via the col-select door), o_proj deferred per row and joined once (b4_tcol + slab join), one t-grid add_rms_norm, one t-row routed-expert sweep (gu_tcol + silu + down8_rows fused down+combine, ONE join per rank), exact t=1 shexp per row. Replaces the layer-major-b1 replay (`[step-tp-batch-exact]`) whose per-row-per-layer recursion kept B=8 flat (69-72 aggregate); boot line `[step-tp-batch-trow]`. Per-session greedy bit-equal to the replay by twin construction. GATED 2026-08-24: TOKFP identity PASS at B=4 (ctx 512, 4/4 streams) and B=8 (ctx 4400, 8/8 streams); aggregate 71.7->73.9 (+3%, B=4 short ctx) and 72.2->80.5 (+11.5%, B=8 at 4400 — the first aggregate movement on the shape every prior receipt had flat at 69-72). LADDER (all identity-gated, sequential arms, delta >> drift band): t-row walk 72.2->80.5, +shexp rows 84.4, +per-session fa rows table 93.3, +compile-time-T tcol twins 95.9 tok/s (83.4 ms/tick, B=8@4400 — 90 crossed on the batch shape; B=4 ctx512 71.2->87.0). FULL CURVE @4400 one binary, identity at every B: B=2 59.9->66.7 (+11%), B=4 67.4->92.0 (+37%), B=6 70.1->97.9 (+40%), B=8 72.2->109.2 (+51%). Remaining named: the per-row head bf16->f32 convert (~4.7 ms/tick, both arms), interleaved x5 before a serving deploy decision. Interleaved x3 PAID (2026-08-24): off 73.6/73.4/73.3 vs on 112.5/111.4/112.5 (+52.5%, drift <0.5%) — deploy-decision-ready. Requires QKV_FUSED + native P2P + all layers step-TP + the big-rig fa ladder; ineligible shapes fall through to the replay. |
| `MEMRA_STEP_NVFP4_EP2` | off | Strict `1`: whole-expert (expert-parallel) NVFP4 banks at 2 ranks — expert `e` lives ENTIRE on rank `e & 1` at bank slot `e >> 1`, REPLACING the TP column/row shards (same total VRAM; the two layouts cannot coexist). Decode rides owner-guarded full-width sweeps (`qmatvec_nvfp4_dp4a_sel_v2_gu_ep`, `silu_mul_scaled_q8_1_sel_ep`, `qmatvec_nvfp4_dp4a_sel_v2_down8_ep` — down+owned-combine fused, per-rank slot-ordered partials, unchanged cross-rank join); prime/oracle ride full-width host-canonical twins (gate/up are BIT-EQUAL to the TP layout — a column shard splits rows, not dots — only down's parenthesization and the combine's regrouping move). NUMERIC-CLASS door (DEV_ROUTES acceptance): run-gen argmax **MATCH** at `logit maxdiff=2.826e-3`. Device-routed decode only; the graph door and the t2 verify driver refuse under it. **RECEIPTED NEGATIVE at B=1 (-4.5%: 72.57/72.59/72.60 vs 75.97/76.03/76.06 at turn8 agentic context, interleaved x3)** — top-8 over 2 owners is binomially imbalanced (expected critical path ~5.1 full experts vs TP's always-balanced 4 full-equivalents), exactly the regime where the vLLM receipt's EP wins do NOT apply (their EP balances under batch). Batch cell landed: B=8 aggregate 69.4 vs base 72.1 (-3.7%) and FLAT vs B=1 in BOTH layouts — the batch tick advances rows through the t=1 step-TP program serially, so no layout ever batches the MoE; the flat-aggregate concurrency receipt measured that serial row loop, not expert-byte physics. The door stays as the EP substrate for a future t-row batched step-TP program. |
